#Jounals:
#je_historic_central_america_falls_apart *Este diario es exclusivo para UCA,Contiene los eventos historicos de Centroamerica 
#je_ca_coffee_boom *Diario sobre el auge del cafe en los paises centroamericanosm,requisitos para disparo que tenga como minimo 5 builds de plantacion de cafe en el estado
#je_reunify_central_america *diario para reunificacion de centroamerica, se dispara por medio de una desicion de jugador/ia le da reclamaciones sobre centroamerica por poco tiempo.
#je_ca_central_american_fragile_unity *diario para la selecion de la faccion que eliga el jugador y en caso del IA;"en este tiene un random_list con en siguente modo: 85% a los federales (historico),15% a los centralistas".
#je_ca_state_role_in_federation *diario sobre la selecion del estado a la faccion que eliga;"federal,centralista,separatista." , este tiene dos modos ; Historico y dinamico.
#je_ca_federalists_vs_separatists_situation *diario exclusivo para GUA,HON,ELS,COS,NIC , en este diario genera un una pelea interna entre federalistas y separatistas, y depende de la selección que hizo en jugador en el diario "je_ca_state_role_in_federation" se dispara eventos historicos/dinamicos sobre esa situacion.
#je_ca_agro_export_boom *diario para el desarrollo economico del estado, de dispara gracias de un evento "aun sin definir".
#je_ca_military_reform *diario para la reforma militar,ya que los paises centroamericanos durante la union federal,tenian un tipo de ley "law_federal_militia" y durante el separatismo "law_regionalist_militia",necesita completar los requisitos para poder reformar la ley,sino cuanto este activo el diario se dispara eventos sobre caudillos,y si falla se cambia la ley a "law_ca_caudillismo". 
#je_ca_enlightenment *diario para desarrollo social y cultural.
#je_ca_company_independence *diario para las companias
#je_ca_company_autonomy *diario para las companias
#je_ca_company_bankruptcy *diario para las companias
#je_ca_national_geological_survey *Con este Diario se podra agregar Minas de hierro,oro,asufre,plumo,oil en los estados de centroamerica,pero tiene un limite "state_trait_mining_gua,state_trait_mining_cos,state_trait_mining_hon,state_trait_mining_els,state_trait_mining_nic,state_trait_mining_pnm",Requisitos: tienes que elegir la decicion de prospeccion para activar este diario, y cuanto este activo se disparara evetos sobre el estudio de los suelos.
#je_ca_historic_situation_in_nicaragua *diario historico para nicaragua,en este se le va la sitiacion al jugador del estado del pais,cuanto este activo se disparara los eventos historicos de nicaragua.
#je_ca_historic_situation_in_costarica *diario historico para costa rica,en este se le va la sitiacion al jugador del estado del pais,cuanto este activo se disparara los eventos historicos de costa rica.
#je_ca_historic_situation_in_talamanca *diario historico para talamanca,en este se le va la sitiacion al jugador del estado del pais,cuanto este activo se disparara los eventos historicos de talamanca.
#je_ca_historic_situation_in_elsalvador *diario historico para el salvador,en este se le va la sitiacion al jugador del estado del pais,cuanto este activo se disparara los eventos historicos de el salvador.
#je_ca_historic_situation_in_guatemala *diario historico para guatemala,en este se le va la sitiacion al jugador del estado del pais,cuanto este activo se disparara los eventos historicos de guatemala.
#je_ca_situation_in_losaltos *diario para los altos.
#je_ca_historic_situation_in_panama *diario historico para panama,en este se le va la sitiacion al jugador del estado del pais,cuanto este activo se disparara los eventos historicos de panama.
#je_ca_historic_situation_in_honduras *diario historico para honduras,en este se le va la sitiacion al jugador del estado del pais,cuanto este activo se disparara los eventos historicos de honduras.
#je_3ter_costa_rica_civil_war *diario para la tercera guerra civil de costa rica 
#je_ca_morazan_situation *Diario sobre Morazan despues de su exilio.

# CAFM F5 (grupo 1): la propuesta del periodico (mayo de 1836).
# Pone la variable global ca_los_altos_question: 1 apoya · 2 censura · 3 rechaza (la lee .120).
ca_central_america.1 = {
    type = country_event
    placement = s:STATE_GUATEMALA.region_state:UCA

    title = ca_central_america.1.t
    desc = ca_central_america.1.d
    flavor = ca_central_america.1.f

    event_image = {
        video = "southamerica_aristocrats"
    }

    icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"
    on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
    duration = 3

    trigger = {
        game_date >= 1836.5.1
        exists = c:UCA
        this = c:UCA
        exists = s:STATE_GUATEMALA.region_state:UCA
        NOT = { has_global_variable = ca_los_altos_proposal_fired }
    }

    immediate = {
        set_global_variable = ca_los_altos_proposal_fired
    }

    # a. Apoyar la propuesta
    option = {
        name = ca_central_america.1.a
        default_option = yes
        set_global_variable = { name = ca_los_altos_question value = 1 }
        ca_frca_bar_moderate_up = yes
        s:STATE_GUATEMALA.region_state:UCA = {
            add_loyalists_in_state = {
                value = 0.1
                culture = cu:central_american
            }
        }
        ai_chance = {
            base = 60
            modifier = {
                trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes }
                add = -50
            }
        }
    }

    # b. Censurar la prensa (sin requisito de ley)
    option = {
        name = ca_central_america.1.b
        set_global_variable = { name = ca_los_altos_question value = 2 }
        s:STATE_GUATEMALA.region_state:UCA = {
            add_modifier = {
                name = ca_newspaper_censored_modifier
                months = 12
            }
        }
        ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { add_modifier = { name = ca_ig_approval_down months = 12 } }
        ai_chance = {
            base = 10
            modifier = {
                trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes }
                add = 50
            }
        }
    }

    # c. No ceder a las quejas provinciales
    option = {
        name = ca_central_america.1.c
        set_global_variable = { name = ca_los_altos_question value = 3 }
        ca_frca_bar_subtract = { VALUE = 5 }
        s:STATE_GUATEMALA.region_state:UCA = {
            add_radicals_in_state = {
                value = 0.15
                culture = cu:central_american
            }
            add_modifier = {
                name = ca_separatist_agitation_modifier
                months = 12
            }
        }
        ai_chance = { base = 30 }
    }
}

## Voting of creation for Los altos
# CAFM F5 (grupo 1): la votacion en el Congreso Federal. Solo la lanza .120 a
# (y su propio aplazamiento). No sale en la ruta centralista.
ca_central_america.2 = {
    type = country_event
    placement = root
    title = ca_central_america.2.t
    desc = ca_central_america.2.d
    flavor = ca_central_america.2.f

    event_image = {
        video = "unspecific_politicians_arguing"
    }
    icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"
    on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
    duration = 3

    trigger = {
        this = c:UCA
        has_global_variable = ca_los_altos_question
        NOT = { exists = c:ALT }
        ca_frca_route_federal = yes
        OR = {
            NOT = { has_global_variable = ca_los_altos_vote_held }
            has_variable = frca_alt_vote_pending
        }
    }

    immediate = {
        set_global_variable = ca_los_altos_vote_held
        remove_variable = frca_alt_vote_pending
    }

    # a. Aprobar (historica): Los Altos sin el Soconusco
    option = {
        name = ca_central_america.2.a
        default_option = yes
        set_variable = { name = los_altos_autonomy_approved_and_granted value = 1 }
        custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.2.approve_tt
        ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
        ig:ig_landowners ?= { add_modifier = { name = ca_ig_approval_down months = 24 } }
        ai_chance = {
            base = 55
            modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
        }
    }

    # b. Rechazar: el altiplano se alza (.4)
    option = {
        name = ca_central_america.2.b
        ca_frca_bar_minor_up = yes
        s:STATE_GUATEMALA.region_state:UCA ?= {
            add_modifier = { name = ca_separatist_agitation_modifier months = 12 }
            add_radicals_in_state = { value = 0.10 pop_type = academics }
            add_radicals_in_state = { value = 0.10 pop_type = shopkeepers }
            add_radicals_in_state = { value = 0.10 pop_type = clerks }
            add_radicals_in_state = { value = 0.10 pop_type = aristocrats }
        }
        set_variable = frca_alt_rebellion_pending
        trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.4 days = 30 }
        ai_chance = { base = 5 }
    }

    # c. Aprobar con el Soconusco: mas territorio, mas tension con Mexico
    option = {
        name = ca_central_america.2.c
        trigger = {
            OR = {
                exists = s:STATE_CHIAPAS.region_state:UCA
                c:CIP ?= { is_subject_of = c:UCA }
            }
        }
        set_variable = { name = los_altos_autonomy_approved_and_granted value = 2 }
        custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.2.approve_soconusco_tt
        ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
        ig:ig_landowners ?= { add_modifier = { name = ca_ig_approval_down months = 24 } }
        c:MEX ?= { change_relations = { country = ROOT value = -20 } }
        ai_chance = { base = 15 }
    }

    # d. Aplazar: una comision. Vuelve en 6 meses; al segundo aplazamiento, .4
    option = {
        name = ca_central_america.2.opt_d # CAFM F7: antes .2.d, que es la descripcion del evento
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_variable = frca_alt_vote_postponed } }
            set_variable = { name = frca_alt_vote_postponed value = 0 }
        }
        change_variable = { name = frca_alt_vote_postponed add = 1 }
        if = {
            limit = { var:frca_alt_vote_postponed >= 2 }
            custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.2.postpone_rebellion_tt
            set_variable = frca_alt_rebellion_pending
            trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.4 days = 30 }
        }
        else = {
            custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.2.postpone_tt
            set_variable = frca_alt_vote_pending
            trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.2 days = 180 }
        }
        ai_chance = {
            base = 25
            modifier = {
                trigger = {
                    has_variable = frca_alt_vote_postponed
                    var:frca_alt_vote_postponed >= 1
                }
                add = -20
            }
        }
    }
}
## Establishment of Los Altos 5 de junio de 1838
# CAFM F5 (grupo 1): establecimiento del Estado de Los Altos (5 jun 1838, Camara de Diputados).
# Sale de .2 a / .2 c. Los Altos nace estado miembro (ca_dependency) con lealtad 3.
# Con la IA historica espera al 5 jun 1838; si no, sale en el siguiente pulso del diario.
ca_central_america.3 = {
    type = country_event
    placement = root
    title = ca_central_america.3.t
    desc = ca_central_america.3.d
    flavor = ca_central_america.3.f

    event_image = {
        video = "unspecific_signed_contract"
    }
    icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_map.dds"
    on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
    duration = 3

    trigger = {
        this = c:UCA
        has_variable = los_altos_autonomy_approved_and_granted
        NOT = { exists = c:ALT }
        NOT = { has_global_variable = ca_los_altos_established }
        OR = {
            NOT = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes }
            game_date >= 1838.6.5
        }
    }

    immediate = {
        set_global_variable = ca_los_altos_established
        if = {
            limit = {
                var:los_altos_autonomy_approved_and_granted = 2
                exists = s:STATE_CHIAPAS.region_state:UCA
            }
            ca_create_losaltoswithsoconusco_effect = yes
        }
        else = {
            ca_create_losaltoswithoutsoconusco_effect = yes
            # Con el Soconusco, pero el Soconusco es de CIP: Los Altos lo absorbe
            if = {
                limit = {
                    var:los_altos_autonomy_approved_and_granted = 2
                    c:CIP ?= { is_subject_of = c:UCA }
                }
                c:ALT ?= { annex = c:CIP }
                set_variable = frca_cip_left_federation
                remove_variable = frca_loyalty_cip
            }
        }
        remove_variable = los_altos_autonomy_approved_and_granted
        c:ALT ?= {
            save_scope_as = scope_ALT
            ca_liberate_losaltos_effect = yes
            change_relations = { country = c:UCA value = 50 }
        }
        ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = alt VALUE = 3 }
    }

    # a. Viva el Sexto Estado!
    option = {
        name = ca_central_america.3.a
        default_option = yes
        ca_frca_bar_minor_up = yes
        ca_frca_loyalty_down = { TAG = gua }
        scope:scope_ALT ?= {
            ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { add_modifier = { name = ca_ig_approval_up months = 24 } }
        }
        ai_chance = { base = 100 }
    }

    # b. Jugar como Los Altos (solo jugador). La UCA pasa a la IA (federal, sorteo 80/20).
    option = {
        name = ca_central_america.3.b
        trigger = { is_ai = no }
        ca_play_losaltos_effect = yes
        ca_frca_selector_leave_uca = yes
        play_as = c:ALT
        ai_chance = { base = 0 }
    }
}
## The vote on the creation of the new state was missed / failed
# CAFM F5 (grupo 1): la rebelion del altiplano. Los Altos nace INDEPENDIENTE (no es
# miembro y no cuenta en el diario). La lanzan .2 b, el 2.o aplazamiento de .2 d,
# .120 b (contador 2) y .120 c, que ponen frca_alt_rebellion_pending.
ca_central_america.4 = {
    type = country_event
    placement = root
    title = ca_central_america.4.t
    desc = ca_central_america.4.d
    flavor = ca_central_america.4.f

    event_image = {
        video = "southamerica_war_civilians"
    }
    icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"
    on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
    duration = 3

    trigger = {
        this = c:UCA
        has_variable = frca_alt_rebellion_pending
        NOT = { exists = c:ALT }
        NOT = { has_global_variable = ca_los_altos_rebellion_fired }
        exists = s:STATE_GUATEMALA.region_state:UCA
    }

    immediate = {
        set_global_variable = ca_los_altos_rebellion_fired
        remove_variable = frca_alt_rebellion_pending
        s:STATE_GUATEMALA.region_state:UCA = {
            save_scope_as = rebellion_state
        }
        create_country = {
            tag = ALT
            origin = ROOT
            province = p:x42124E
            province = p:x78C465
            province = p:xD9BEFE
            province = p:xBD8628
            on_created = {
                set_variable = { name = ca_secession_country }
                save_scope_as = scope_ALT
                capital = { save_scope_as = secession_capital }
            }
        }
        scope:scope_ALT ?= {
            ca_liberate_losaltos_effect = yes
        }
    }

    # a. Aceptar el hecho consumado
    option = {
        name = ca_central_america.4.a
        scope:scope_ALT ?= {
            change_relations = { country = c:UCA value = 20 }
        }
        ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
        ai_chance = {
            base = 40
            modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -30 }
            modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -40 }
        }
    }

    # b. Recuperar el altiplano: guerra de anexion; si la UCA gana, vuelve a Guatemala
    option = {
        name = ca_central_america.4.b
        ca_frca_loyalty_up = { TAG = gua }
        ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
        scope:scope_ALT ?= {
            change_relations = { country = c:UCA value = -50 }
        }
        create_diplomatic_play = {
            name = ca_secession_war
            type = dp_annex_war
            target_country = scope:scope_ALT
        }
        ai_chance = {
            base = 20
            modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 50 }
            modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -20 }
        }
    }

    # c. Que Guatemala lo resuelva (historica): la UCA no actua. Cuando Guatemala se
    # separe recibe la reclamacion y puede reabsorberlo (Carrera, ene 1840).
    # Frente conservador +1 al empezar la fase IV.
    option = {
        name = ca_central_america.4.c
        default_option = yes
        set_global_variable = ca_gua_claims_los_altos
        set_variable = frca_alt_left_to_guatemala
        custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.4.c_tt
        ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
        ai_chance = {
            base = 40
            modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -20 }
            modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
        }
    }
}

## The Fate of Soconusco
# CAFM F14: reescrito
ca_central_america.5 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.5.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.5.d
				trigger = {
					has_variable = ca_soconusco_result_central
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.5.d2
				trigger = {
					always = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = ca_central_america.5.f
	event_image = { video = "asia_farmers_market" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_map.dds"
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:CIP
		OR = {
			has_variable = ca_soconusco_result_central
			has_variable = ca_soconusco_result_mexico
		}
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_soconusco_fate_decided }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.5.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = {
			has_variable = ca_soconusco_result_central
			is_subject_of = c:UCA
		}
		set_variable = ca_soconusco_fate_decided
		c:UCA ?= { ca_frca_loyalty_up = { TAG = cip } }
		add_modifier = { name = ca_federal_protection_modifier months = 120 }
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.5.b
		trigger = {
			has_variable = ca_soconusco_result_central
			exists = c:GUA
			c:GUA = { is_subject = no }
		}
		set_variable = ca_soconusco_fate_decided
		c:UCA ?= { set_variable = guatemala_integrate_soconusco }
		set_global_variable = guatemala_integrate_soconusco
		if = {
			limit = { is_subject_of = c:UCA }
			c:UCA = { remove_diplomatic_pact = { country = c:CIP type = ca_dependency } }
		}
		c:GUA = { annex = c:CIP }
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.5.c
		trigger = {
			has_variable = ca_soconusco_result_central
			exists = c:ALT
		}
		set_variable = ca_soconusco_fate_decided
		c:UCA ?= { set_variable = quetzaltenango_integrate_soconusco }
		set_global_variable = quetzaltenango_integrate_soconusco
		if = {
			limit = { is_subject_of = c:UCA }
			c:UCA = { remove_diplomatic_pact = { country = c:CIP type = ca_dependency } }
		}
		c:ALT = { annex = c:CIP }
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.5.opt_d
		trigger = {
			has_variable = ca_soconusco_result_central
			is_ai = no
		}
		set_variable = ca_soconusco_fate_decided
		if = {
			limit = { is_subject_of = c:UCA }
			c:UCA = { remove_diplomatic_pact = { country = c:CIP type = ca_dependency } }
			make_independent = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.5.e
		trigger = {
			has_variable = ca_soconusco_result_mexico
			exists = c:MEX
		}
		set_variable = ca_soconusco_fate_decided
		set_global_variable = ca_soconusco_lost
		s:STATE_CHIAPAS = { remove_homeland = cu:central_american }
		if = {
			limit = { is_subject_of = c:UCA }
			c:UCA = { remove_diplomatic_pact = { country = c:CIP type = ca_dependency } }
		}
		c:MEX = { annex = c:CIP }
		c:GUA ?= { trigger_event = { id = ca_los_altos_soconusco_events.5 days = 1 } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = c:GUA } }
			c:UCA ?= { trigger_event = { id = ca_los_altos_soconusco_events.5 days = 1 } }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 60
		modifier = { trigger = { has_global_variable = ca_world_historic } add = 500 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.5.opt_f
		trigger = {
			has_variable = ca_soconusco_result_mexico
			exists = c:MEX
		}
		set_variable = ca_soconusco_fate_decided
		if = {
			limit = { is_subject_of = c:UCA }
			c:UCA = { remove_diplomatic_pact = { country = c:CIP type = ca_dependency } }
			make_independent = yes
		}
		c:MEX = {
			create_diplomatic_play = {
				name = ca_soconusco_war_play
				target_country = c:CIP
				type = dp_annex_war
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 20
		modifier = { trigger = { has_global_variable = ca_world_historic } factor = 0 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.5.g
		trigger = {
			has_variable = ca_soconusco_result_mexico
		}
		set_variable = ca_soconusco_fate_decided
		set_global_variable = ca_soconusco_lost
		c:GUA ?= { trigger_event = { id = ca_los_altos_soconusco_events.5 days = 1 } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = c:GUA } }
			c:UCA ?= { trigger_event = { id = ca_los_altos_soconusco_events.5 days = 1 } }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.5.h
		trigger = {
			has_variable = ca_soconusco_result_mexico
			NOT = { exists = c:MEX }
		}
		set_variable = ca_soconusco_fate_decided
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
}

ca_central_america.6 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.6.t
	desc = ca_central_america.6.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.6.f
	event_image = { video = "africa_construction_colony" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_map.dds"
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		NOT = { exists = c:COS }
		NOT = { has_global_variable = ca_bocas_intro_fired }
		game_date >= 1836.2.1
		game_date < 1837.1.1
		p:x47C5F8.state.owner ?= c:UCA
		exists = c:GBR
		exists = c:CLM
		OR = {
			c:MKT ?= { is_subject_of = c:GBR }
			c:GBR = { has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_central_america }
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_global_variable = ca_bocas_intro_fired
		set_global_variable = bocas_del_toro_crisis_fired
		set_global_variable = { name = ca_bocas_control value = 20 } # P4: control efectivo de Bocas
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.6.a
		default_option = yes
		c:GBR = { trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.7 days = 60 } }
		c:CLM = { trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.8 days = 320 } }
		custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.6.a.tt
		ca_bocas_control_change = { VALUE = 5 }
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	# P4 (punto 5): mas opciones para la UCA
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.6.opt_b
		trigger = { gold_reserves >= ca_money_medium }
		add_treasury = { value = ca_money_medium multiply = -1 }
		set_variable = { name = ca_bocas_garrison days = 730 }
		ca_bocas_control_change = { VALUE = 25 }
		c:GBR = { trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.7 days = 60 } }
		c:CLM = { trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.8 days = 320 } }
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.6.opt_c
		trigger = { gold_reserves >= ca_money_large }
		add_treasury = { value = ca_money_large multiply = -1 }
		ca_bocas_control_change = { VALUE = 35 }
		ca_cohesion_change = { VALUE = -5 }
		c:GBR = { trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.7 days = 60 } }
		c:CLM = { trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.8 days = 320 } }
		ai_chance = { base = 15 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.6.opt_d
		set_global_variable = ca_bocas_native_pact
		ca_bocas_control_change = { VALUE = 15 }
		ca_cohesion_change = { VALUE = -5 }
		c:GBR = { trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.7 days = 60 } }
		c:CLM = { trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.8 days = 320 } }
		ai_chance = { base = 15 }
	}
}

ca_central_america.7 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.7.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.7.d
				trigger = {
					c:UCA ?= { has_journal_entry = je_ca_british_debt }
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.7.d2
				trigger = {
					always = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = ca_central_america.7.f
	event_image = { video = "europenorthamerica_london_center" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_map.dds"
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:GBR
		has_global_variable = ca_bocas_intro_fired
		NOT = { has_global_variable = ca_bocas_gbr_decided }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.7.a
		default_option = yes
		set_global_variable = ca_bocas_gbr_decided
		c:UCA ?= { change_relations = { country = root value = 10 } }
		ai_chance = { base = 80 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.7.b
		set_global_variable = ca_bocas_gbr_decided
		if = {
			limit = {
			OR = {
				p:x47C5F8.state.owner ?= c:UCA
				p:x47C5F8.state.owner ?= c:PP1
			}
			NOT = { ca_bocas_control_high = yes } # P4: con control alto la usurpacion fracasa
			}
		s:STATE_PANAMA = {
			set_owner_of_provinces = {
				country = root
				provinces = { x47C5F8 }
			}
		}
			set_global_variable = ca_bocas_taken_gbr
			if = {
				limit = { c:UCA ?= { has_journal_entry = je_ca_british_debt } }
				c:UCA = { add_treasury = { value = ca_money_medium } }
				custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.7.debt_tt
			}
			c:UCA ?= {
				change_relations = { country = root value = -30 }
				ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
		s:STATE_PANAMA = { add_claim = root }
		p:x47C5F8.state.owner ?= {
			change_relations = { country = root value = -30 }
		}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 20 modifier = { trigger = { OR = { has_global_variable = ca_bocas_native_pact has_global_variable = ca_bocas_protested } } add = -40 } modifier = { trigger = { ca_bocas_control_high = yes } add = -60 } }
	}
}

		name = ca_central_america.8.a

			limit = {
			OR = {
				p:x47C5F8.state.owner ?= c:UCA

			}
		}

				change_relations = { country = root value = -30 }
				trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.12 days = 15 }
			}
			trigger_event = { id = ca_panama_events.2 days = 30 }
		}
		else = {
		s:STATE_PANAMA = { add_claim = root }

			change_relations = { country = root value = -30 }
		}
		}

	}

		set_global_variable = ca_bocas_clm_decided
		c:UCA ?= { change_relations = { country = root value = -10 } }

	}
}

	}

		name = ca_central_america.9.a

			tag = PP1
			origin = root
			province = p:x47C5F8

				set_variable = ca_bocas_de_toro_country
			}
		}
		c:PP1 ?= {
			root = {
				create_diplomatic_pact = {
					country = c:PP1
					type = ca_dependency
				}
			}

				limit = { has_global_variable = cafm_hc_galindo }
				global_var:cafm_hc_galindo = {
					transfer_character = c:PP1
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
				}
			}
		}
		change_infamy = 5
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes
		ai_chance = { base = 40
		modifier = { trigger = { has_variable = frca_ai_historic } factor = 0 } }
	}

## The Fate of Bocas de Toro (Resolution)
# CAFM F14: reescrito
ca_central_america.11 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.11.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.11.d
				trigger = {
					this = c:UCA
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.11.d2
				trigger = {
					always = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = ca_central_america.11.f
	event_image = { video = "africa_construction_colony" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_map.dds"
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			this = c:UCA
			this = c:GBR
			this = c:CLM
			this = c:USA
		}
		exists = c:PP1
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_bocas_11_fired }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.11.uca_a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = {
			this = c:UCA
		}
		set_variable = ca_bocas_11_fired
		c:PP1 ?= {
			if = {
				limit = { is_subject_of = c:UCA }
				c:UCA = { remove_diplomatic_pact = { country = c:PP1 type = ca_dependency } }
				make_independent = yes
			}
		}
		s:STATE_PANAMA = { add_claim = c:UCA }
		ai_chance = { base = 50
		modifier = { trigger = { has_variable = frca_ai_historic } add = 500 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.11.uca_b
		trigger = {
			this = c:UCA
		}
		set_variable = ca_bocas_11_fired
		if = {
			limit = { var:ca_bocas_aid >= 2 }
			set_global_variable = ca_bocas_secured
			c:PP1 ?= { add_modifier = { name = ca_bocas_federal_protection months = 60 } }
			ca_frca_bar_minor_up = yes
		}
		else = {
			custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.11.weak_tt
			add_treasury = { value = ca_money_small multiply = -1 }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 50
		modifier = { trigger = { has_variable = frca_ai_historic } factor = 0 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.11.gbr_a
		trigger = {
			this = c:GBR
		}
		set_variable = ca_bocas_11_fired
		ai_chance = { base = 80 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.11.gbr_b
		trigger = {
			this = c:GBR
		}
		set_variable = ca_bocas_11_fired
		if = {
			limit = {
			c:PP1 ?= {
				NOT = { has_global_variable = ca_bocas_secured }
				NOT = { is_subject_of = c:GBR }
				NOT = { is_subject_of = c:CLM }
			}
			}
			c:PP1 = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_subject_of = c:UCA }
					c:UCA = { remove_diplomatic_pact = { country = c:PP1 type = ca_dependency } }
				}
			}
			create_diplomatic_pact = {
				country = c:PP1
				type = protectorate
			}
			c:UCA ?= { change_relations = { country = root value = -30 } }
		}
		else = {
			custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.11.too_late_tt
			c:PP1 ?= { change_relations = { country = root value = -20 } }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.11.clm_a
		trigger = {
			this = c:CLM
		}
		set_variable = ca_bocas_11_fired
		if = {
			limit = {
			c:PP1 ?= {
				NOT = { has_global_variable = ca_bocas_secured }
				NOT = { is_subject_of = c:GBR }
				NOT = { is_subject_of = c:CLM }
			}
			}
			annex = c:PP1
			set_global_variable = ca_bocas_taken_clm
			c:UCA ?= {
				change_relations = { country = root value = -30 }
				trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.12 days = 15 }
			}
		}
		else = {
			custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.11.too_late_tt
			s:STATE_PANAMA = { add_claim = root }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 80 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.11.clm_b
		trigger = {
			this = c:CLM
		}
		set_variable = ca_bocas_11_fired
		c:PP1 ?= { change_relations = { country = root value = 30 } }
		c:UCA ?= { change_relations = { country = root value = 20 } }
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.11.usa_a
		trigger = {
			this = c:USA
		}
		set_variable = ca_bocas_11_fired
		ai_chance = { base = 80 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.11.usa_b
		trigger = {
			this = c:USA
		}
		set_variable = ca_bocas_11_fired
		c:PP1 ?= { change_relations = { country = root value = 40 } }
		set_global_variable = ca_bocas_usa_treaty
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
}

## Dissolution of Central America
ca_central_america.13 = {
    type = country_event
    placement = root
    title = ca_central_america.13.t
    desc = ca_central_america.13.d
    flavor = ca_central_america.13.f
    duration = 3
    
    event_image = { video = "ep1_transfer_of_authority" }
    icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"
    on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
    
    trigger = {
        this = c:UCA
        NOT = { has_global_variable = ca_dissolution_fired } # Freno de seguridad
    }

    immediate = {
        set_global_variable = ca_dissolution_fired
        
        # Guardamos la región de la capital de la UCA para saber quiénes somos
        capital = {
            state_region = { save_scope_as = capital_region_scope }
        }
		ca_all_indepedient = yes
		ca_remove_ideological_strongholds_effect = yes
        ca_remove_political_movements_effect = yes 
        # ==========================================
        # FASE 1: SECESIÓN DE LOS DEMÁS ESTADOS O LIBERACIÓN
        # ==========================================
        if = {
		limit = { has_journal_entry = central_america_falls_apart}
		ca_dissolution_vainilla = yes
		}
		
		# --- LOS ALTOS (ALT) sin Soconusco ---
		if = {
		    limit = {
		        NOT = { has_variable = quetzaltenango_integrate_soconusco }
		        NOT = { exists = c:ALT }
		        #p:x42124E = { owner = UCA }
		    }
		    create_country = {
		        tag = ALT
		        origin = ROOT
		        province = p:x42124E
		        province = p:x78C465
		        province = p:xD9BEFE
		        province = p:xBD8628                
		        on_created = {
		            ca_liberate_losaltos_effect = yes
					set_variable = { name = ca_secession_country }
		        }
		    }
			c:ALT = { make_independent = yes }
		}
		
		
		
		# --- LOS ALTOS (ALT) con Soconusco ---
		if = {
		    limit = {
		        has_variable = quetzaltenango_integrate_soconusco
		        NOT = { exists = c:ALT }
		        exists = s:STATE_CHIAPAS.region_state:UCA
		        NOT = { scope:capital_region_scope = s:STATE_CHIAPAS }
		    }
		    create_country = {
		        tag = ALT
		        origin = ROOT
		        state = s:STATE_CHIAPAS.region_state:UCA
		        province = p:x42124E
		        province = p:x78C465
		        province = p:xD9BEFE
		        province = p:xBD8628                
		        on_created = {
		            ca_liberate_losaltos_effect = yes
					set_variable = { name = ca_secession_country }
		        }
		    }
			c:ALT = { make_independent = yes }
		}

        # --- SOCONUSCO (CIP) ---
        if = {
            limit = {
				NOT = { exists = c:CIP }
                exists = s:STATE_CHIAPAS.region_state:UCA
                NOT = { scope:capital_region_scope = s:STATE_CHIAPAS }
            }
            s:STATE_CHIAPAS = {
                add_homeland = cu:chiapanecan
                remove_homeland = cu:central_american   
            } 
            create_country = {
                tag = CIP
                origin = ROOT                
                province = p:x1030C0 
                province = p:x4DBDE9 
                province = p:xD03040 
                province = p:xA087F6
                province = p:x8E26C5
                on_created = {                   
					set_variable = is_soconosco
					set_variable = { name = ca_secession_country }
                }
            }
			c:CIP = { make_independent = yes }
        }   
            
        # --- GUATEMALA (GUA) sin Soconusco ---
        if = {
            limit = {
                exists = s:STATE_GUATEMALA.region_state:UCA
                NOT = { scope:capital_region_scope = s:STATE_GUATEMALA }
                NOT = { has_variable = guatemala_integrate_soconusco }
            }
            s:STATE_GUATEMALA = {
                add_homeland = cu:guatemalan
                remove_homeland = cu:central_american   
            }            
            create_country = {
                tag = GUA
                origin = ROOT
                state = s:STATE_GUATEMALA.region_state:UCA
                province = p:x31E49A
				province = p:x40B001 
				province = p:xCE98DE
                on_created = {
                    add_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
                    remove_primary_culture = cu:central_american
                    ca_liberate_guatemala_effect = yes
					set_variable = { name = ca_secession_country }
                }
            }
			c:GUA = { make_independent = yes }
        }

        # --- GUATEMALA (GUA) con Soconusco ---
        if = {
            limit = {
                exists = s:STATE_GUATEMALA.region_state:UCA
                NOT = { scope:capital_region_scope = s:STATE_GUATEMALA }
                has_variable = guatemala_integrate_soconusco
            }
            s:STATE_GUATEMALA = {
                add_homeland = cu:guatemalan
                remove_homeland = cu:central_american   
            }
            create_country = {
                tag = GUA
                origin = ROOT
                state = s:STATE_GUATEMALA.region_state:UCA
				province = p:x31E49A
				province = p:x40B001 
				province = p:xCE98DE
                on_created = {
                    add_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
                    remove_primary_culture = cu:central_american
                    ca_liberate_guatemala_effect = yes
					set_variable = { name = ca_secession_country }
                }
            }
			c:GUA = { make_independent = yes }
            # Traspaso oficial de Soconusco a GUA
            if = {
                limit = { exists = s:STATE_CHIAPAS.region_state:GUA }
                s:STATE_CHIAPAS = { 
                    set_owner_of_provinces = {
                        country = scope:scope_GUA
                        provinces = { x1030C0 x4DBDE9 xD03040 xA087F6 x8E26C5 }
                    }
                }
            }
        }


        # --- HONDURAS (HON) ---
        if = {
            limit = {
                exists = s:STATE_HONDURAS.region_state:UCA
                NOT = { scope:capital_region_scope = s:STATE_HONDURAS }
            }
            s:STATE_HONDURAS = {
                add_homeland = cu:honduran
                remove_homeland = cu:central_american
            }            
            create_country = {
                tag = HON
                origin = ROOT
                state = s:STATE_HONDURAS.region_state:UCA
                on_created = {
                    add_primary_culture = cu:honduran
                    remove_primary_culture = cu:central_american
                    ca_liberate_honduras_effect = yes
					set_variable = { name = ca_secession_country }
                }
            }
			c:HON = { make_independent = yes }
        }

        # --- COSTA RICA (COS) ---
        if = {
            limit = {
                exists = s:STATE_COSTA_RICA.region_state:UCA
                NOT = { scope:capital_region_scope = s:STATE_COSTA_RICA }
            }
            s:STATE_COSTA_RICA = {
                add_homeland = cu:costa_rican
                remove_homeland = cu:central_american            
            }            
            create_country = {
                tag = COS
                origin = ROOT
                state = s:STATE_COSTA_RICA.region_state:UCA
				province = p:x47C5F8
                on_created = {
                    add_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
                    remove_primary_culture = cu:central_american
                    ca_liberate_costarica_effect = yes
					set_variable = { name = ca_secession_country }
					c:COS ?= {
			        create_diplomatic_pact = {
			        country = c:RDT
			        type = ca_autonomous_province
			        }
		            }
                }
            }
			c:COS = { make_independent = yes }
        }

        # --- EL SALVADOR (ELS) ---
        if = {
            limit = {
                exists = s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR.region_state:UCA
                NOT = { scope:capital_region_scope = s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR }
            }
            s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR = {
                add_homeland = cu:el_salvadorian
                remove_homeland = cu:central_american
            }            
            create_country = {
                tag = ELS
                origin = ROOT
                state = s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR.region_state:UCA
                on_created = {
                    add_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
                    remove_primary_culture = cu:central_american
                    ca_liberate_elsalvador_effect = yes
					set_variable = { name = ca_secession_country }
                }
            }
			c:ELS = { make_independent = yes }
        }

        # --- NICARAGUA (NIC) ---
        if = {
            limit = {
                exists = s:STATE_NICARAGUA.region_state:UCA
                NOT = { scope:capital_region_scope = s:STATE_NICARAGUA }
            }
            s:STATE_NICARAGUA = {
                add_homeland = cu:nicaraguan
                remove_homeland = cu:central_american
            }            
            create_country = {
                tag = NIC
                origin = ROOT
                state = s:STATE_NICARAGUA.region_state:UCA
                on_created = {
                    add_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
                    remove_primary_culture = cu:central_american
                    ca_liberate_nicaragua_effect = yes
					set_variable = { name = ca_secession_country }
                }
            }
			c:NIC = { make_independent = yes }
        }
		## --- PANAMA (PNM) ---
        #if = { 
        #    limit = {
        #        exists = s:STATE_PANAMA.region_state:UCA
        #        NOT = { scope:capital_region_scope = s:STATE_PANAMA }
        #    }
        #    s:STATE_PANAMA = {
        #        add_homeland = cu:panamanian
        #        remove_homeland = cu:central_american
        #    }            
        #    create_country = {
        #        tag = PNM
        #        origin = ROOT
        #        state = s:STATE_PANAMA.region_state:UCA
        #        on_created = {
        #            add_primary_culture = cu:panamanian
        #            remove_primary_culture = cu:central_american
        #            #ca_liberate_panama_effect = yes
		#            set_variable = { name = ca_secession_country }
        #        }
        #    }
		#	c:PNM = { make_independent = yes }
        #}

		
     	 
        
				 	
				 			
				 		
		
        # ==========================================
        # FASE 2: PREPARAR AL ESTADO CAPITAL (NUESTRO PAÍS)
        # ==========================================
        if = {
            limit = { scope:capital_region_scope = s:STATE_NICARAGUA }
            s:STATE_NICARAGUA = { add_homeland = cu:nicaraguan remove_homeland = cu:central_american }
            add_primary_culture = cu:nicaraguan
            remove_primary_culture = cu:central_american
        }
        if = {
            limit = { scope:capital_region_scope = s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR }
            s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR = { add_homeland = cu:el_salvadorian remove_homeland = cu:central_american }
            add_primary_culture = cu:el_salvadorian
            remove_primary_culture = cu:central_american
        }
        if = {
            limit = { scope:capital_region_scope = s:STATE_COSTA_RICA }
            s:STATE_COSTA_RICA = { add_homeland = cu:costa_rican remove_homeland = cu:central_american }
            add_primary_culture = cu:costa_rican
            remove_primary_culture = cu:central_american
        }
        if = {
            limit = { scope:capital_region_scope = s:STATE_HONDURAS }
            s:STATE_HONDURAS = { add_homeland = cu:honduran remove_homeland = cu:central_american }
            add_primary_culture = cu:honduran
            remove_primary_culture = cu:central_american
        }
        if = {
            limit = { scope:capital_region_scope = s:STATE_GUATEMALA }
            s:STATE_GUATEMALA = { add_homeland = cu:guatemalan remove_homeland = cu:central_american }
            add_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
            remove_primary_culture = cu:central_american
        }
        
        if = {
            limit = { 
                NOT = { exists = c:ALT }
                scope:capital_region_scope = s:STATE_CHIAPAS # Asumiendo Quetzaltenango o Soconusco
            }
            add_primary_culture = cu:guatemalan
            remove_primary_culture = cu:central_american
        }
    }

    # ========================================================
    # OPCIÓN A: Aceptación Pacífica. Dejarlos ir.
    # ========================================================
    option = {
        name = ca_central_america.13.a
        default_option = yes
                    
        
           if = {limit = {NOT = { exists = c:NIC }capital = {state_region = s:STATE_NICARAGUA}}change_tag = NIC}
           if = {limit = {NOT = { exists = c:ELS }capital = {state_region = s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR}}change_tag = ELS}
           if = {limit = {NOT = { exists = c:COS }capital = {state_region = s:STATE_COSTA_RICA}}change_tag = COS}
           if = {limit = {NOT = { exists = c:HON }capital = {state_region = s:STATE_HONDURAS}}change_tag = HON}
           if = {limit = {NOT = { exists = c:GUA }capital = {state_region = s:STATE_GUATEMALA}}change_tag = GUA}
           if = {limit = {NOT = { exists = c:ALT }capital = {state_region = s:STATE_CHIAPAS}}change_tag = ALT}

		capital = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
				pop_type = laborers
			}
		}        
        capital = {
                                
                                                     
            add_loyalists_in_state = {
                value = 0.5
                culture = cu:central_american
            }
        }
        
        every_country = {
            limit = { has_variable = ca_secession_country }
			change_relations = {
				country = ROOT
				value = 20
			}	 
            capital = {
                add_devastation = 15 
                add_loyalists_in_state = {
                    value = 0.5
                    culture = cu:central_american
                }
            }
        }
		ca_remove_setup_ig_ideologies_post_uca = yes
    	ai_chance = { base = 90 } # F21
    }

    # ========================================================
    # OPCIÓN B: Luchar hasta el final (Reconquista por la Fuerza)
    # ========================================================
    option = { 
        name = ca_central_america.13.b
        add_journal_entry = { type = je_ca_patria_grande } # F12: antes el diario vanilla je_reunify_central_america
        hidden_effect = { ca_remove_ideological_strongholds_effect = yes }
		if = { limit = { scope:capital_region_scope = s:STATE_NICARAGUA } change_tag= NIC }
        if = { limit = { scope:capital_region_scope = s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR } change_tag= ELS }
        if = { limit = { scope:capital_region_scope = s:STATE_COSTA_RICA } change_tag= COS }
        if = { limit = { scope:capital_region_scope = s:STATE_HONDURAS } change_tag= HON }
        if = { limit = { scope:capital_region_scope = s:STATE_GUATEMALA } change_tag= GUA }
        
        # Odio, guerra y reclamos sobre los separatistas
             every_country = {
                limit = { has_variable = ca_secession_country }
                change_relations = { country = ROOT value = -50 }
                # La Región Estatal reclama para la UCA (ROOT)
				capital = {
				        state_region = {
				        	add_claim = ROOT
				        	add_devastation = 25
				        }
                }
                

            }
    	ai_chance = { base = 10 } # F21
    }
}
## The Livingston Penal Code
# CAFM F16: reescrito
ca_central_america.14 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.14.t
	desc = ca_central_america.14.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.14.f
	event_image = { video = "europenorthamerica_capitalists_meeting" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		ca_frca_journal_active = yes
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_livingston_applied }
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_livingston_rejected }
		game_date >= 1836.8.1
		game_date < 1837.1.1
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.14.a
		default_option = yes
		set_variable = ca_livingston_applied
		add_modifier = { name = uca_new_penal_code months = 120 }
		ig:ig_landowners ?= { add_modifier = { name = ca_ig_approval_down months = 36 } }
		ig:ig_devout ?= { add_modifier = { name = ca_ig_approval_down months = 36 } }
		ca_frca_loyalty_down = { TAG = gua }
		ai_chance = { base = 70
		modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 20 }
		modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -40 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.14.b
		set_variable = ca_livingston_rejected
		add_modifier = { name = ca_reforms_stalled months = 24 }
		ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { add_modifier = { name = ca_ig_approval_down months = 24 } }
		ai_chance = { base = 30
		modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -20 }
		modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 40 } }
	}
}

## Cholera Outbreak in Guatemala
# CAFM F16: reescrito
ca_central_america.15 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.15.t
	desc = ca_central_america.15.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.15.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_sick_in_hospital" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			this = c:UCA
			this = c:GUA
		}
		NOT = { has_global_variable = ca_gua_cholera_fired }
		game_date >= 1837.2.1
		s:STATE_GUATEMALA = {
			any_scope_state = {
				owner = root
				NOT = { has_modifier = uca_cholera }
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_global_variable = ca_gua_cholera_fired
		s:STATE_GUATEMALA = {
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = { owner = root }
				save_scope_as = ca_cholera_gua
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.15.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:ca_cholera_gua = {
			add_modifier = { name = uca_cholera days = 365 }
			add_modifier = { name = uca_quarantine days = 370 }
		}
		set_global_variable = ca_carrera_revolt_seed
		if = {
			limit = { this = c:UCA }
			ca_frca_loyalty_down = { TAG = gua }
			ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		}
		else = {
			add_radicals = { value = small_radicals strata = lower }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 50
		modifier = { trigger = { OR = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes has_global_variable = ca_world_historic } } add = 400 }
		modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { is_in_government = yes } } add = 20 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.15.b
		scope:ca_cholera_gua = {
			add_modifier = { name = uca_cholera days = 365 }
			add_radicals_in_state = { value = 0.15 pop_type = peasants }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30
		modifier = { trigger = { OR = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes has_global_variable = ca_world_historic } } factor = 0.1 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.15.c
		scope:ca_cholera_gua = {
			add_modifier = { name = uca_cholera days = 365 }
			add_modifier = { name = ca_cholera_clergy days = 365 }
		}
		ig:ig_devout ?= { add_modifier = { name = ca_ig_approval_up months = 24 } }
		ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { add_modifier = { name = ca_ig_approval_down months = 24 } }
		ai_chance = { base = 20
		modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_devout ?= { is_in_government = yes } } add = 20 } }
	}
}

## Generic Cholera Outbreak in Central America
# CAFM F16: reescrito
ca_central_america.16 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.16.t
	desc = ca_central_america.16.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.16.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_sick_in_hospital" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		game_date >= 1836.1.1
		game_date < 1880.1.1
		has_law = law_type:law_no_health_system
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_generic_cholera_cd }
		any_scope_state = {
			region = sr:region_central_america
			NOT = { has_variable = ca_generic_cholera }
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				region = sr:region_central_america
				NOT = { has_variable = ca_generic_cholera }
			}
			save_scope_as = ca_cholera_state
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.16.a
		default_option = yes
		ca_generic_cholera_cooldown = yes
		scope:ca_cholera_state = {
			set_variable = { name = ca_generic_cholera days = 365 }
			add_modifier = { name = ca_generic_cholera_mod days = 365 }
			add_modifier = { name = uca_quarantine days = 370 }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.16.b
		ca_generic_cholera_cooldown = yes
		scope:ca_cholera_state = {
			set_variable = { name = ca_generic_cholera days = 365 }
			add_modifier = { name = ca_generic_cholera_mod days = 365 }
			add_radicals_in_state = { value = 0.1 pop_type = peasants }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
}

## Cholera Outbreak Spreads
# CAFM F16: reescrito
ca_central_america.17 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.17.t
	desc = ca_central_america.17.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.17.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_sick_in_hospital" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3
	cooldown = { months = 4 }

	trigger = {
		any_scope_state = {
				region = sr:region_central_america
				has_variable = ca_generic_cholera
				NOT = { has_modifier = uca_quarantine }
				any_neighbouring_state = {
					owner = root
					NOT = { has_variable = ca_generic_cholera }
				}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				region = sr:region_central_america
				has_variable = ca_generic_cholera
				NOT = { has_modifier = uca_quarantine }
				any_neighbouring_state = {
					owner = root
					NOT = { has_variable = ca_generic_cholera }
				}
			}
			random_neighbouring_state = {
				limit = {
					owner = root
					NOT = { has_variable = ca_generic_cholera }
				}
				save_scope_as = ca_cholera_spread
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.17.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:ca_cholera_spread = {
			set_variable = { name = ca_generic_cholera days = 365 }
			add_modifier = { name = ca_generic_cholera_mod days = 365 }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.17.b
		scope:ca_cholera_spread = {
			set_variable = { name = ca_generic_cholera days = 365 }
			add_modifier = { name = ca_generic_cholera_mod days = 365 }
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = ca_generic_cholera
				NOT = { has_modifier = uca_quarantine }
			}
			add_modifier = { name = uca_quarantine days = 370 }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
}


## Separatism in Costa Rica
# CAFM F6 (grupo 4): separatismo en Costa Rica (paso 1). Fecha historica 1838.9.15; paso 2 1838.11.15.
# V1 (la UCA): el estado sigue siendo provincia de la UCA; se crea como subdito de la IA.
# V2 (el estado): ya es miembro (jugador o IA); no sale si eligio apoyar a los separatistas.
ca_central_america.18 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.18.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.18.d
				trigger = { this = c:UCA }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.18.d2
				trigger = { always = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = ca_central_america.18.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_politicians_arguing" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_global_variable = ca_sep_cos_fired }
		ca_frca_journal_active = yes
		OR = {
			# V1
			AND = {
				this = c:UCA
				NOT = { exists = c:COS }
				exists = s:STATE_COSTA_RICA.region_state:UCA
				OR = {
					# Historico (IA 80 %): en su fecha, sin exigir lealtad baja
					AND = {
						ca_frca_ai_historic = yes
						game_date >= 1838.9.15
					}
					# Dinamico (jugador o IA 20 %): fecha, lealtad <= 1 y ventana hasta el paso 2
					AND = {
						NOT = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes }
						NOT = { c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_press_watch } } # F9: prensa vigilada
						game_date >= 1838.9.15
						OR = {
							game_date < 1838.11.15
							c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_sep_negotiated_cos }
						}
						NOT = { c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_sep_cooldown_cos } }
						ca_frca_loyalty_le = { TAG = cos VALUE = 1 }
						NOT = { ca_frca_phase_ge = { VALUE = 4 } }
					}
				}
			}
			# V2
			AND = {
				c:COS ?= this
				is_subject_of = c:UCA
				NOT = { has_variable = ca_role_separatist }
				OR = {
					# Historico (IA 80 %): en su fecha, sin exigir lealtad baja
					AND = {
						ca_frca_ai_historic = yes
						game_date >= 1838.9.15
					}
					# Dinamico (jugador o IA 20 %): fecha, lealtad <= 1 y ventana hasta el paso 2
					AND = {
						NOT = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes }
						NOT = { c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_press_watch } } # F9: prensa vigilada
						game_date >= 1838.9.15
						OR = {
							game_date < 1838.11.15
							c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_sep_negotiated_cos }
						}
						NOT = { c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_sep_cooldown_cos } }
						ca_frca_loyalty_le = { TAG = cos VALUE = 1 }
						NOT = { ca_frca_phase_ge = { VALUE = 4 } }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	# --- V1: la UCA ---
	# a. Reconocer su autonomia (historica)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.18.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { this = c:UCA }
		set_global_variable = ca_sep_cos_fired
		ca_frca_secession_create = { TAG = cos CTAG = COS NAME = costarica }
		c:COS ?= { change_relations = { country = c:UCA value = 20 } }
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes
		ca_frca_secession_reaction = { CTAG = COS }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -40 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
		}
	}
	# b. Negociar concesiones: no se separa todavia; vuelve en 12 meses si la lealtad cae a <= 1
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.18.b
		trigger = { this = c:UCA }
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = cos VALUE = 2 }
		s:STATE_COSTA_RICA.region_state:UCA ?= {
			add_modifier = { name = ca_autonomy_granted months = 24 }
		}
		set_variable = frca_sep_negotiated_cos
		set_variable = { name = frca_sep_cooldown_cos days = 365 }
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -40 }
		}
	}
	# c. Mano dura: se crea el estado y estalla su levantamiento armado
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.18.c
		trigger = { this = c:UCA }
		set_global_variable = ca_sep_cos_fired
		ca_frca_secession_create = { TAG = cos CTAG = COS NAME = costarica }
		c:COS ?= { change_relations = { country = c:UCA value = -50 } }
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes
		set_variable = frca_cos_armed_pending
		trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.66 days = 15 popup = yes }
		ca_frca_secession_reaction = { CTAG = COS }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 60 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -10 }
		}
	}

	# --- V2: el estado ---
	# a. Seguir al movimiento (historica)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.18.v2a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { NOT = { this = c:UCA } }
		set_global_variable = ca_sep_cos_fired
		ca_role_add_journal = yes
		ca_fvs_add_journal = yes
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = cos VALUE = 0 }
		ca_ai_costarica_effect = yes
		c:UCA ?= {
			ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes
			ca_frca_secession_reaction = { CTAG = COS }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_landowners ?= { is_in_government = yes } } add = 30 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
		}
	}
	# b. Contener a los separatistas
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.18.v2b
		trigger = { NOT = { this = c:UCA } }
		ca_frca_loyalty_up = { TAG = cos }
		every_scope_state = {
			add_modifier = { name = ca_separatist_agitation_modifier months = 12 }
		}
		add_radicals = { value = small_radicals }
		c:UCA ?= { set_variable = { name = frca_sep_cooldown_cos days = 365 } }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_loyalty_ge = { TAG = cos VALUE = 2 } } add = 20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -30 }
		}
	}
	# c. Pedir intervencion federal (.28-.33, F7)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.18.v2c
		trigger = { NOT = { this = c:UCA } }
		set_variable = { name = ca_intervention_request_active days = 180 }
		c:UCA ?= {
			set_variable = { name = frca_sep_cooldown_cos days = 365 }
			trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.29 days = 1 popup = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { is_in_government = yes } } add = 20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -20 }
		}
	}
}

## Separatism in Nicaragua 
# CAFM F6 (grupo 4): separatismo en Nicaragua (paso 1). Fecha historica 1838.2.28; paso 2 1838.4.30.
# V1 (la UCA): el estado sigue siendo provincia de la UCA; se crea como subdito de la IA.
# V2 (el estado): ya es miembro (jugador o IA); no sale si eligio apoyar a los separatistas.
ca_central_america.19 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.19.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.19.d
				trigger = { this = c:UCA }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.19.d2
				trigger = { always = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = ca_central_america.19.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_politicians_arguing" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_global_variable = ca_sep_nic_fired }
		ca_frca_journal_active = yes
		OR = {
			# V1
			AND = {
				this = c:UCA
				NOT = { exists = c:NIC }
				exists = s:STATE_NICARAGUA.region_state:UCA
				OR = {
					# Historico (IA 80 %): en su fecha, sin exigir lealtad baja
					AND = {
						ca_frca_ai_historic = yes
						game_date >= 1838.2.28
					}
					# Dinamico (jugador o IA 20 %): fecha, lealtad <= 1 y ventana hasta el paso 2
					AND = {
						NOT = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes }
						NOT = { c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_press_watch } } # F9: prensa vigilada
						OR = {
							game_date >= 1838.2.28
							AND = {
								# CAFM F7: tras una intervencion rechazada (.41), 3 meses antes
								c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_sep_early_nic }
								game_date >= 1837.11.28
							}
						}
						OR = {
							game_date < 1838.4.30
							c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_sep_negotiated_nic }
						}
						NOT = { c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_sep_cooldown_nic } }
						ca_frca_loyalty_le = { TAG = nic VALUE = 1 }
						NOT = { ca_frca_phase_ge = { VALUE = 4 } }
					}
				}
			}
			# V2
			AND = {
				c:NIC ?= this
				is_subject_of = c:UCA
				NOT = { has_variable = ca_role_separatist }
				OR = {
					# Historico (IA 80 %): en su fecha, sin exigir lealtad baja
					AND = {
						ca_frca_ai_historic = yes
						game_date >= 1838.2.28
					}
					# Dinamico (jugador o IA 20 %): fecha, lealtad <= 1 y ventana hasta el paso 2
					AND = {
						NOT = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes }
						NOT = { c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_press_watch } } # F9: prensa vigilada
						OR = {
							game_date >= 1838.2.28
							AND = {
								# CAFM F7: tras una intervencion rechazada (.41), 3 meses antes
								c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_sep_early_nic }
								game_date >= 1837.11.28
							}
						}
						OR = {
							game_date < 1838.4.30
							c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_sep_negotiated_nic }
						}
						NOT = { c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_sep_cooldown_nic } }
						ca_frca_loyalty_le = { TAG = nic VALUE = 1 }
						NOT = { ca_frca_phase_ge = { VALUE = 4 } }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	# --- V1: la UCA ---
	# a. Reconocer su autonomia (historica)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.19.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { this = c:UCA }
		set_global_variable = ca_sep_nic_fired
		ca_frca_secession_create = { TAG = nic CTAG = NIC NAME = nicaragua }
		c:NIC ?= { change_relations = { country = c:UCA value = 20 } }
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes
		ca_frca_secession_reaction = { CTAG = NIC }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -40 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
		}
	}
	# b. Negociar concesiones: no se separa todavia; vuelve en 12 meses si la lealtad cae a <= 1
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.19.b
		trigger = { this = c:UCA }
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = nic VALUE = 2 }
		s:STATE_NICARAGUA.region_state:UCA ?= {
			add_modifier = { name = ca_autonomy_granted months = 24 }
		}
		set_variable = frca_sep_negotiated_nic
		set_variable = { name = frca_sep_cooldown_nic days = 365 }
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -40 }
		}
	}
	# c. Mano dura: se crea el estado y estalla su levantamiento armado
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.19.c
		trigger = { this = c:UCA }
		set_global_variable = ca_sep_nic_fired
		ca_frca_secession_create = { TAG = nic CTAG = NIC NAME = nicaragua }
		c:NIC ?= { change_relations = { country = c:UCA value = -50 } }
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes
		set_variable = frca_nic_armed_pending
		trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.64 days = 15 popup = yes }
		ca_frca_secession_reaction = { CTAG = NIC }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 60 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -10 }
		}
	}

	# --- V2: el estado ---
	# a. Seguir al movimiento (historica)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.19.v2a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { NOT = { this = c:UCA } }
		set_global_variable = ca_sep_nic_fired
		ca_role_add_journal = yes
		ca_fvs_add_journal = yes
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = nic VALUE = 0 }
		ca_ai_nicaragua_effect = yes
		c:UCA ?= {
			ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes
			ca_frca_secession_reaction = { CTAG = NIC }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_landowners ?= { is_in_government = yes } } add = 30 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
		}
	}
	# b. Contener a los separatistas
	option = {

		ca_frca_loyalty_up = { TAG = nic }
		every_scope_state = {
			add_modifier = { name = ca_separatist_agitation_modifier months = 12 }
		}
		add_radicals = { value = small_radicals }
		c:UCA ?= { set_variable = { name = frca_sep_cooldown_nic days = 365 } }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_loyalty_ge = { TAG = nic VALUE = 2 } } add = 20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -30 }
		}
	}
	# c. Pedir intervencion federal (.28-.33, F7)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.19.v2c
		trigger = { NOT = { this = c:UCA } }
		set_variable = { name = ca_intervention_request_active days = 180 }
		c:UCA ?= {
			set_variable = { name = frca_sep_cooldown_nic days = 365 }
			trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.28 days = 1 popup = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { is_in_government = yes } } add = 20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -20 }
		}
	}
}

## Separatism in Guatemala

	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.20.t
	desc = {

			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.20.d
				trigger = { this = c:UCA }

				this = c:UCA

						NOT = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes }
						NOT = { c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_press_watch } } # F9: prensa vigilada
						game_date >= 1839.2.17
						OR = {
							game_date < 1839.4.17
							c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_sep_negotiated_gua }

		}
	}

	# --- V1: la UCA ---
	# a. Reconocer su autonomia (historica)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.20.a
		default_option = yes

	}
