	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.20.b
		trigger = { this = c:UCA }
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = gua VALUE = 2 }
		s:STATE_GUATEMALA.region_state:UCA ?= {
			add_modifier = { name = ca_autonomy_granted months = 24 }
		}
		set_variable = frca_sep_negotiated_gua
		set_variable = { name = frca_sep_cooldown_gua days = 365 }
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -40 }
		}
	}
	# c. Mano dura: se crea el estado y estalla su levantamiento armado
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.20.c
		trigger = { this = c:UCA }
		set_global_variable = ca_sep_gua_fired
		ca_frca_secession_create = { TAG = gua CTAG = GUA NAME = guatemala }
		c:GUA ?= { change_relations = { country = c:UCA value = -50 } }
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes
		set_variable = frca_gua_armed_pending
		trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.70 days = 15 popup = yes }
		ca_frca_secession_reaction = { CTAG = GUA }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 60 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -10 }
		}
	}

	# --- V2: el estado ---
	# a. Seguir al movimiento (historica)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.20.v2a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { NOT = { this = c:UCA } }
		set_global_variable = ca_sep_gua_fired
		ca_role_add_journal = yes
		ca_fvs_add_journal = yes
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = gua VALUE = 0 }
		ca_ai_guatemala_effect = yes
		c:UCA ?= {
			ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes
			ca_frca_secession_reaction = { CTAG = GUA }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_landowners ?= { is_in_government = yes } } add = 30 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
		}
	}
	# b. Contener a los separatistas
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.20.v2b
		trigger = { NOT = { this = c:UCA } }
		ca_frca_loyalty_up = { TAG = gua }
		every_scope_state = {
			add_modifier = { name = ca_separatist_agitation_modifier months = 12 }
		}
		add_radicals = { value = small_radicals }
		c:UCA ?= { set_variable = { name = frca_sep_cooldown_gua days = 365 } }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_loyalty_ge = { TAG = gua VALUE = 2 } } add = 20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -30 }
		}
	}
	# c. Pedir intervencion federal (.28-.33, F7)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.20.v2c
		trigger = { NOT = { this = c:UCA } }
		set_variable = { name = ca_intervention_request_active days = 180 }
		c:UCA ?= {
			set_variable = { name = frca_sep_cooldown_gua days = 365 }
			trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.32 days = 1 popup = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { is_in_government = yes } } add = 20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -20 }
		}
	}
}

## Separatism in Honduras  
# CAFM F6 (grupo 4): separatismo en Honduras (paso 1). Fecha historica 1838.8.26; paso 2 1838.10.26.
# V1 (la UCA): el estado sigue siendo provincia de la UCA; se crea como subdito de la IA.
# V2 (el estado): ya es miembro (jugador o IA); no sale si eligio apoyar a los separatistas.
ca_central_america.21 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.21.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.21.d
				trigger = { this = c:UCA }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.21.d2
				trigger = { always = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = ca_central_america.21.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_politicians_arguing" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_global_variable = ca_sep_hon_fired }
		ca_frca_journal_active = yes
		OR = {
			# V1
			AND = {
				this = c:UCA
				NOT = { exists = c:HON }
				exists = s:STATE_HONDURAS.region_state:UCA
				OR = {
					# Historico (IA 80 %): en su fecha, sin exigir lealtad baja
					AND = {
						ca_frca_ai_historic = yes
						game_date >= 1838.8.26
					}
					# Dinamico (jugador o IA 20 %): fecha, lealtad <= 1 y ventana hasta el paso 2
					AND = {
						NOT = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes }
						NOT = { c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_press_watch } } # F9: prensa vigilada
						OR = {
							game_date >= 1838.8.26
							AND = {
								# CAFM F7: tras una intervencion rechazada (.41), 3 meses antes
								c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_sep_early_hon }
								game_date >= 1838.5.26
							}
						}
						OR = {
							game_date < 1838.10.26
							c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_sep_negotiated_hon }
						}
						NOT = { c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_sep_cooldown_hon } }
						ca_frca_loyalty_le = { TAG = hon VALUE = 1 }
						NOT = { ca_frca_phase_ge = { VALUE = 4 } }
					}
				}
			}
			# V2
			AND = {
				c:HON ?= this
				is_subject_of = c:UCA
				NOT = { has_variable = ca_role_separatist }
				OR = {
					# Historico (IA 80 %): en su fecha, sin exigir lealtad baja
					AND = {
						ca_frca_ai_historic = yes
						game_date >= 1838.8.26
					}
					# Dinamico (jugador o IA 20 %): fecha, lealtad <= 1 y ventana hasta el paso 2
					AND = {
						NOT = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes }
						NOT = { c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_press_watch } } # F9: prensa vigilada
						OR = {
							game_date >= 1838.8.26
							AND = {
								# CAFM F7: tras una intervencion rechazada (.41), 3 meses antes
								c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_sep_early_hon }
								game_date >= 1838.5.26
							}
						}
						OR = {
							game_date < 1838.10.26
							c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_sep_negotiated_hon }
						}
						NOT = { c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_sep_cooldown_hon } }
						ca_frca_loyalty_le = { TAG = hon VALUE = 1 }
						NOT = { ca_frca_phase_ge = { VALUE = 4 } }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	# --- V1: la UCA ---
	# a. Reconocer su autonomia (historica)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.21.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { this = c:UCA }
		set_global_variable = ca_sep_hon_fired
		ca_frca_secession_create = { TAG = hon CTAG = HON NAME = honduras }
		c:HON ?= { change_relations = { country = c:UCA value = 20 } }
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes
		ca_frca_secession_reaction = { CTAG = HON }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -40 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
		}
	}
	# b. Negociar concesiones: no se separa todavia; vuelve en 12 meses si la lealtad cae a <= 1
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.21.b
		trigger = { this = c:UCA }
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = hon VALUE = 2 }
		s:STATE_HONDURAS.region_state:UCA ?= {
			add_modifier = { name = ca_autonomy_granted months = 24 }
		}
		set_variable = frca_sep_negotiated_hon
		set_variable = { name = frca_sep_cooldown_hon days = 365 }
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -40 }
		}
	}
	# c. Mano dura: se crea el estado y estalla su levantamiento armado
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.21.c
		trigger = { this = c:UCA }
		set_global_variable = ca_sep_hon_fired
		ca_frca_secession_create = { TAG = hon CTAG = HON NAME = honduras }
		c:HON ?= { change_relations = { country = c:UCA value = -50 } }
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes
		set_variable = frca_hon_armed_pending
		trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.68 days = 15 popup = yes }
		ca_frca_secession_reaction = { CTAG = HON }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 60 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -10 }
		}
	}

	# --- V2: el estado ---
	# a. Seguir al movimiento (historica)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.21.v2a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { NOT = { this = c:UCA } }
		set_global_variable = ca_sep_hon_fired
		ca_role_add_journal = yes
		ca_fvs_add_journal = yes
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = hon VALUE = 0 }
		ca_ai_honduras_effect = yes
		c:UCA ?= {
			ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes
			ca_frca_secession_reaction = { CTAG = HON }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_landowners ?= { is_in_government = yes } } add = 30 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
		}
	}
	# b. Contener a los separatistas
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.21.v2b
		trigger = { NOT = { this = c:UCA } }
		ca_frca_loyalty_up = { TAG = hon }
		every_scope_state = {
			add_modifier = { name = ca_separatist_agitation_modifier months = 12 }
		}
		add_radicals = { value = small_radicals }
		c:UCA ?= { set_variable = { name = frca_sep_cooldown_hon days = 365 } }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_loyalty_ge = { TAG = hon VALUE = 2 } } add = 20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -30 }
		}
	}
	# c. Pedir intervencion federal (.28-.33, F7)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.21.v2c
		trigger = { NOT = { this = c:UCA } }
		set_variable = { name = ca_intervention_request_active days = 180 }
		c:UCA ?= {
			set_variable = { name = frca_sep_cooldown_hon days = 365 }
			trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.30 days = 1 popup = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { is_in_government = yes } } add = 20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -20 }
		}
	}
}

## Separatism in El Salvador
# CAFM F6 (grupo 4): separatismo en El Salvador (paso 1). Fecha historica 1840.11.30; paso 2 1841.1.30.
# V1 (la UCA): el estado sigue siendo provincia de la UCA; se crea como subdito de la IA.
# V2 (el estado): ya es miembro (jugador o IA); no sale si eligio apoyar a los separatistas.
ca_central_america.22 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.22.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.22.d
				trigger = { this = c:UCA }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.22.d2
				trigger = { always = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = ca_central_america.22.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_politicians_arguing" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_global_variable = ca_sep_els_fired }
		ca_frca_journal_active = yes
		# El Salvador solo se separa si la capital de la UCA no esta en San Salvador (grupo 11)
		c:UCA ?= { capital = { NOT = { state_region = s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR } } }
		OR = {
			# V1
			AND = {
				this = c:UCA
				NOT = { exists = c:ELS }
				exists = s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR.region_state:UCA
				OR = {
					# Historico (IA 80 %): en su fecha, sin exigir lealtad baja
					AND = {
						ca_frca_ai_historic = yes
						game_date >= 1840.11.30
					}
					# Dinamico (jugador o IA 20 %): fecha, lealtad <= 1 y ventana hasta el paso 2
					AND = {
						NOT = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes }
						NOT = { c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_press_watch } } # F9: prensa vigilada
						game_date >= 1840.11.30
						OR = {
							game_date < 1841.1.30
							c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_sep_negotiated_els }
						}
						NOT = { c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_sep_cooldown_els } }
						ca_frca_loyalty_le = { TAG = els VALUE = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
			# V2
			AND = {
				c:ELS ?= this
				is_subject_of = c:UCA
				NOT = { has_variable = ca_role_separatist }
				OR = {
					# Historico (IA 80 %): en su fecha, sin exigir lealtad baja
					AND = {
						ca_frca_ai_historic = yes
						game_date >= 1840.11.30
					}
					# Dinamico (jugador o IA 20 %): fecha, lealtad <= 1 y ventana hasta el paso 2
					AND = {
						NOT = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes }
						NOT = { c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_press_watch } } # F9: prensa vigilada
						game_date >= 1840.11.30
						OR = {
							game_date < 1841.1.30
							c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_sep_negotiated_els }
						}
						NOT = { c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_sep_cooldown_els } }
						ca_frca_loyalty_le = { TAG = els VALUE = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	# --- V1: la UCA ---
	# a. Reconocer su autonomia (historica)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.22.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { this = c:UCA }
		set_global_variable = ca_sep_els_fired
		ca_frca_secession_create = { TAG = els CTAG = ELS NAME = elsalvador }
		c:ELS ?= { change_relations = { country = c:UCA value = 20 } }
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes
		ca_frca_secession_reaction = { CTAG = ELS }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -40 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
		}
	}
	# b. Negociar concesiones: no se separa todavia; vuelve en 12 meses si la lealtad cae a <= 1
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.22.b
		trigger = { this = c:UCA }
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = els VALUE = 2 }
		s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR.region_state:UCA ?= {
			add_modifier = { name = ca_autonomy_granted months = 24 }
		}
		set_variable = frca_sep_negotiated_els
		set_variable = { name = frca_sep_cooldown_els days = 365 }
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -40 }
		}
	}
	# c. Mano dura: se crea el estado y estalla su levantamiento armado
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.22.c
		trigger = { this = c:UCA }
		set_global_variable = ca_sep_els_fired
		ca_frca_secession_create = { TAG = els CTAG = ELS NAME = elsalvador }
		c:ELS ?= { change_relations = { country = c:UCA value = -50 } }
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes
		set_variable = frca_els_armed_pending
		trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.72 days = 15 popup = yes }
		ca_frca_secession_reaction = { CTAG = ELS }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 60 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -10 }
		}
	}

	# --- V2: el estado ---
	# a. Seguir al movimiento (historica)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.22.v2a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { NOT = { this = c:UCA } }
		set_global_variable = ca_sep_els_fired
		ca_role_add_journal = yes
		ca_fvs_add_journal = yes
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = els VALUE = 0 }
		ca_ai_elsalvador_effect = yes
		c:UCA ?= {
			ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes
			ca_frca_secession_reaction = { CTAG = ELS }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_landowners ?= { is_in_government = yes } } add = 30 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
		}
	}
	# b. Contener a los separatistas
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.22.v2b
		trigger = { NOT = { this = c:UCA } }
		ca_frca_loyalty_up = { TAG = els }
		every_scope_state = {
			add_modifier = { name = ca_separatist_agitation_modifier months = 12 }
		}
		add_radicals = { value = small_radicals }
		c:UCA ?= { set_variable = { name = frca_sep_cooldown_els days = 365 } }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_loyalty_ge = { TAG = els VALUE = 2 } } add = 20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -30 }
		}
	}
	# c. Pedir intervencion federal (.28-.33, F7)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.22.v2c
		trigger = { NOT = { this = c:UCA } }
		set_variable = { name = ca_intervention_request_active days = 180 }
		c:UCA ?= {
			set_variable = { name = frca_sep_cooldown_els days = 365 }

		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { is_in_government = yes } } add = 20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -20 }
		}
	}

## Separatism in Los Altos
# CAFM F6 (grupo 4, C): la cuestion de Los Altos. Solo V2: la crisis de Los Altos ya miembro,
# que lleva a su independencia (.58 / .78). Solo dinamico (sin fecha historica).
ca_central_america.23 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.23.t
	desc = ca_central_america.23.d2
	flavor = ca_central_america.23.f

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

		NOT = { has_global_variable = ca_sep_alt_fired }
		ca_frca_journal_active = yes
		c:ALT ?= this
		is_subject_of = c:UCA

		NOT = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes }
		NOT = { c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_press_watch } } # F9: prensa vigilada
		NOT = { c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_sep_cooldown_alt } }

		every_scope_state = {
			add_modifier = { name = ca_separatist_agitation_modifier months = 12 }

			base = 30

			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -30 }
		}
	}
	# c. Pedir intervencion federal (.28-.33, F7)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.23.v2c
		trigger = { NOT = { this = c:UCA } }
		set_variable = { name = ca_intervention_request_active days = 180 }
		c:UCA ?= {
			set_variable = { name = frca_sep_cooldown_alt days = 365 }
			trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.33 days = 1 popup = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { is_in_government = yes } } add = 20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -20 }
		}
	}

## Opposition to secession
# CAFM F6 (grupo 5): oposicion a la secesion. Dinamico; lo lanza ca_frca_secession_reaction
# a la UCA y al estado. Cada uno ve sus opciones. Abre la puerta a la guerra civil del estado
# (opposition_crisis_var en el estado), sin provocarla de forma automatica.
ca_central_america.24 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.24.t
	desc = ca_central_america.24.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.24.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_politicians_arguing" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		has_variable = ca_reaction_target
		exists = var:ca_reaction_target
	}

	immediate = {
		var:ca_reaction_target = { save_scope_as = secession_country }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { this = c:UCA } }
			# Lider unionista en el estado
			create_character = {
				ig_leader = yes
				interest_group = ig_intelligentsia
				ideology = ideology_ca_federalist
			}
		}
	}

	# UCA a. Apoyar a los manifestantes
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.24.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { this = c:UCA }
		add_treasury = { value = ca_money_small multiply = -1 }
		scope:secession_country = {
			set_variable = opposition_crisis_var
			change_relations = { country = c:UCA value = -20 }
		}
		ca_frca_bar_minor_up = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 40 }
		}
	}
	# UCA b. Pedir calma y orden
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.24.b
		trigger = { this = c:UCA }
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -40 }
		}
	}
	# Estado a. Reprimir a los unionistas
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.24.c
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { NOT = { this = c:UCA } }
		set_variable = opposition_crisis_var
		add_radicals = { value = small_radicals }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_landowners ?= { is_in_government = yes } } add = 20 }
		}
	}
	# Estado b. Negociar con ellos: puede volver a la Federacion
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.24.e
		trigger = { NOT = { this = c:UCA } }
		ca_state_loyalty_change = { VALUE = 1 }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { is_in_government = yes } } add = 20 }
		}
	}
	after = { remove_variable = ca_reaction_target }
}

## Acceptance of secession
# CAFM F6 (grupo 5): aceptacion de la secesion. Dinamico; a la UCA y al estado.
ca_central_america.25 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.25.t
	desc = ca_central_america.25.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.25.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_signed_contract" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		has_variable = ca_reaction_target
		exists = var:ca_reaction_target
	}

	immediate = {
		var:ca_reaction_target = { save_scope_as = secession_country }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { this = c:UCA } }
			# Lider moderado de los terratenientes en el estado
			create_character = {
				ig_leader = yes
				interest_group = ig_landowners
				ideology = ideology_moderate
			}
		}
	}

	# UCA a. Es la voluntad del pueblo
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.25.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { this = c:UCA }
		scope:secession_country = {
			change_relations = { country = c:UCA value = 20 }
			set_variable = ca_no_civil_war
		}
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -40 }
		}
	}
	# UCA b. Es una traicion cobarde
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.25.b
		trigger = { this = c:UCA }
		scope:secession_country = {
			change_relations = { country = c:UCA value = -30 }
			set_variable = opposition_crisis_var
			set_variable = ca_intervention_allowed
		}
		ca_frca_bar_minor_up = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 40 }
		}
	}
	# Estado a. Consolidar la separacion
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.25.c
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { NOT = { this = c:UCA } }
		add_modifier = { name = ca_secession_consolidated months = 60 }
		ai_chance = { base = 60 }
	}
	# Estado b. Ofrecer un pacto de amistad a la Federacion (candidato a la Confederacion)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.25.e
		trigger = { NOT = { this = c:UCA } }
		change_relations = { country = c:UCA value = 30 }
		set_variable = ca_confederation_candidate
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { is_in_government = yes } } add = 20 }
		}
	}
	after = { remove_variable = ca_reaction_target }
}

## Nicaragua declares independence on Arms
# CAFM F6 (grupo 11): declaracion de independencia de Costa Rica (lado del estado, solo dinamico).
# Tras *Falls* (subdito ca_dependency con lealtad 0). Tambien vuelve si la UCA niega la peticion.
ca_central_america.26 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.26.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.26.d_denied
				trigger = { has_variable = ca_independence_denied }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.26.d
				trigger = { always = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = ca_central_america.26.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_signed_contract" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		c:COS ?= this
		is_subject_of = c:UCA
		is_subject_type = subject_type_ca_dependency
		ca_frca_loyalty_le = { TAG = cos VALUE = 0 }
		NOT = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes }
		is_at_war = no
		OR = {
			has_variable = ca_independence_denied
			AND = {
				ca_frca_phase_ge = { VALUE = 2 }
				NOT = { ca_frca_phase_ge = { VALUE = 4 } }
				NOT = { has_variable = ca_independence_declared }
				NOT = { has_variable = ca_declaration_wait }
				NOT = { has_journal_entry = je_ca_federalists_vs_separatists_situation }
				NOT = { has_variable = ca_fvs_result_beats }
			}
		}
	}

	# a. Declararla pacificamente: peticion a la UCA
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.26.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { NOT = { has_variable = ca_independence_denied } }
		set_variable = ca_independence_declared
		c:UCA ?= {
			set_variable = frca_cos_independence_request
			trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.45 days = 7 popup = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
	# b. Declararla en armas
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.26.b
		set_variable = ca_independence_declared
		remove_variable = ca_independence_denied
		c:UCA ?= {
			set_variable = frca_cos_armed_pending
			trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.66 days = 7 popup = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { is_powerful = yes } } add = 20 }
		}
	}
	# c. Esperar: vuelve en 6 meses
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.26.c
		trigger = { NOT = { has_variable = ca_independence_denied } }
		set_variable = { name = ca_declaration_wait days = 180 }
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
	# d. Someterse (tras la negativa de la UCA)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.26.d_opt
		trigger = { has_variable = ca_independence_denied }
		remove_variable = ca_independence_denied
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = cos VALUE = 1 }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { is_powerful = yes } } add = -20 }
		}
	}
}

## Nicaragua declares independence Peaceful
# CAFM F6 (grupo 11): declaracion de independencia de Nicaragua (lado del estado, solo dinamico).

#Nicaragua apply intervention
# CAFM F7 (grupo 6): Nicaragua pide la intervencion federal. Fuentes: .19 c (el estado) y el
# boton ca_request_intervention_button (estado de la IA en modo dinamico). Fases II-III; una
# peticion por estado cada 12 meses.
ca_central_america.28 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.28.t
	desc = ca_central_america.28.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.28.f
	event_image = { video = "europenorthamerica_before_the_battle" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		c:NIC ?= {
			has_variable = ca_intervention_request_active
			is_subject_of = c:UCA
		}
		NOT = { has_variable = frca_interv_cooldown_nic }
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_intervention_cooldown_var }
		NOT = { ca_frca_phase_ge = { VALUE = 4 } }
	}

	immediate = {
		c:NIC ?= {
			remove_variable = ca_intervention_request_active
			save_scope_as = ca_target
		}
		set_variable = { name = frca_interv_cooldown_nic days = 365 }
	}

	# a. Enviar el ejercito federal: resultado de la campana (.37)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.28.a
		default_option = yes
		if = {
			limit = { c:NIC ?= { has_variable = nic_pidio_federacion } }
			add_treasury = -5000 # F15 (E1): Nicaragua pidio la tutela federal
		}
		else = {
			add_treasury = -10000
		}
		trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.37 days = 30 popup = yes }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 10 }
			modifier = { trigger = { gold_reserves < 10000 } add = -40 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -60 }
		}
	}

	# b. Enviar dinero y armas
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.28.b
		add_treasury = -5000
		scope:ca_target = {
			add_modifier = { name = ca_federal_intervention_boost months = 12 }
			cafm_catalyst_fire = { TYPE = catalyst_ca_federal_aid_accepted TARGET = c:UCA }
		}
		ca_frca_bar_minor_up = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -15 }
			modifier = { trigger = { gold_reserves < 5000 } add = -20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -25 }
		}
	}

	# c. Ignorar la peticion
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.28.c
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes
		ca_frca_loyalty_down = { TAG = nic }
		scope:ca_target = {
			cafm_catalyst_fire = { TYPE = catalyst_ca_federal_aid_refused TARGET = c:UCA }
		}
		set_variable = { name = frca_refused_target value = scope:ca_target }
		trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.41 days = 1 popup = yes }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 15
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 5 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
		}
	}
}

		}

		trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.41 days = 1 popup = yes }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 15
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 5 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
		}

		ca_frca_loyalty_down = { TAG = els }
		scope:ca_target = {
			cafm_catalyst_fire = { TYPE = catalyst_ca_federal_aid_refused TARGET = c:UCA }
		}
		set_variable = { name = frca_refused_target value = scope:ca_target }
		trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.41 days = 1 popup = yes }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 15
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 5 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -15 }
		}
	}
}

#Guatemala apply intervention
# CAFM F7 (grupo 6): Guatemala pide la intervencion federal. Fuentes: .20 c (el estado) y el
# boton ca_request_intervention_button (estado de la IA en modo dinamico). Fases II-III; una
# peticion por estado cada 12 meses.
ca_central_america.32 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.32.t
	desc = ca_central_america.32.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.32.f
	event_image = { video = "europenorthamerica_before_the_battle" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		c:GUA ?= {
			has_variable = ca_intervention_request_active
			is_subject_of = c:UCA
		}
		NOT = { has_variable = frca_interv_cooldown_gua }
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_intervention_cooldown_var }
		NOT = { ca_frca_phase_ge = { VALUE = 4 } }
	}

	immediate = {
		c:GUA ?= {
			remove_variable = ca_intervention_request_active
			save_scope_as = ca_target
		}
		set_variable = { name = frca_interv_cooldown_gua days = 365 }
	}

	# a. Enviar el ejercito federal: resultado de la campana (.39)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.32.a
		default_option = yes
		add_treasury = -10000
		trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.39 days = 30 popup = yes }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 10 }
			modifier = { trigger = { gold_reserves < 10000 } add = -40 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
		}
	}

	# b. Enviar dinero y armas
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.32.b
		add_treasury = -5000
		scope:ca_target = {
			add_modifier = { name = ca_federal_intervention_boost months = 12 }
			cafm_catalyst_fire = { TYPE = catalyst_ca_federal_aid_accepted TARGET = c:UCA }
		}
		ca_frca_bar_minor_up = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -15 }
			modifier = { trigger = { gold_reserves < 5000 } add = -20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -25 }
		}
	}

	# c. Ignorar la peticion
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.32.c
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes
		ca_frca_loyalty_down = { TAG = gua }
		scope:ca_target = {
			cafm_catalyst_fire = { TYPE = catalyst_ca_federal_aid_refused TARGET = c:UCA }
		}
		set_variable = { name = frca_refused_target value = scope:ca_target }
		trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.41 days = 1 popup = yes }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 15
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 5 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -15 }
		}
	}
}

## Panama apply intervention
# CAFM F14: reescrito
ca_central_america.34 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.34.t
	desc = ca_central_america.34.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.34.f
	event_image = { video = "africa_diplomats_negotiating" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_diplomacy.dds"
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			this = c:UCA
			this = c:COS
		}
		exists = c:PNM
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_isthmus_34_fired }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.34.a
		default_option = yes
		set_variable = ca_isthmus_34_fired
		set_global_variable = ca_pnm_supported
		c:PNM = { change_relations = { country = root value = 30 } }
		c:CLM ?= { change_relations = { country = root value = -30 } }
		if = {
			limit = { this = c:UCA }
			ca_frca_bar_minor_up = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30
		modifier = { trigger = { has_global_variable = ca_world_historic } factor = 0 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.34.b
		set_variable = ca_isthmus_34_fired
		if = {
			limit = { this = c:UCA }
			ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 70 }
	}
}
## UCA Accept the intervention in Costa Rica 
# CAFM F7 (grupo 7): resultado de la intervencion federal en Costa Rica.
# Exito: 50 % + 10 % por nivel de lealtad +- ejercito y ajuste del estado (ca_intervention_success_chance).
# Con la IA historica se fuerza el resultado real.
ca_central_america.35 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.35.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.35.d_ok
				trigger = { has_variable = frca_interv_success }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.35.d_ko
				trigger = { always = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = ca_central_america.35.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_military_parade" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		exists = c:COS
	}

	immediate = {
		remove_variable = frca_interv_success
		c:COS = { save_scope_as = ca_target }
		random = { chance = ca_intervention_success_chance set_variable = frca_interv_success }
	}

	# --- Exito ---
	# a. Dejar una guarnicion federal
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.35.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { has_variable = frca_interv_success }
		ca_frca_loyalty_change = { TAG = cos VALUE = 2 }
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_up = yes
		add_treasury = -5000
		set_variable = { name = frca_loyalty_min2_cos days = 365 }
		scope:ca_target = { add_modifier = { name = ca_federal_intervention_boost months = 12 } }
		scope:ca_target = { set_variable = cos_carrillo_deposed }
		custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.35.ok_tt
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = { trigger = { gold_reserves < 5000 } add = -40 }
		}
	}
	# b. Retirar las tropas
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.35.b
		trigger = { has_variable = frca_interv_success }
		ca_frca_loyalty_change = { TAG = cos VALUE = 1 }
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_up = yes
		scope:ca_target = { add_modifier = { name = ca_federal_intervention_boost months = 12 } }
		scope:ca_target = { set_variable = cos_carrillo_deposed }
		custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.35.ok_tt
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}

	# --- Fracaso ---
	# a. Retirarse
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.35.c
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { NOT = { has_variable = frca_interv_success } }
		ca_frca_loyalty_down = { TAG = cos }
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		scope:ca_target = { set_variable = cos_carrillo_strengthened }
		ai_chance = { base = 60 }
	}
	# b. Enviar refuerzos: otra tirada; si vuelve a fallar, crisis y guerra civil del estado
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.35.e
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_variable = frca_interv_success }
			NOT = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes }
		}
		add_treasury = -10000
		random = { chance = ca_intervention_success_chance set_variable = frca_interv_reinforced }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = frca_interv_reinforced }
			remove_variable = frca_interv_reinforced
			custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.reinforce_ok_tt
			ca_frca_loyalty_change = { TAG = cos VALUE = 1 }
			ca_frca_bar_moderate_up = yes
			scope:ca_target = { add_modifier = { name = ca_federal_intervention_boost months = 12 } }
		}
		else = {
			custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.reinforce_ko_tt
			ca_frca_loyalty_down = { TAG = cos }
			ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
			scope:ca_target = { set_variable = opposition_crisis_var }
		scope:ca_target = { set_variable = cos_carrillo_strengthened }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = { trigger = { gold_reserves < 10000 } add = -40 }
		}
	}
}

## UCA Accept the intervention in Honduras 
# CAFM F7 (grupo 7): resultado de la intervencion federal en Honduras.
# Exito: 50 % + 10 % por nivel de lealtad +- ejercito y ajuste del estado (ca_intervention_success_chance).
# Con la IA historica se fuerza el resultado real.
ca_central_america.36 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.36.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.36.d_ok
				trigger = { has_variable = frca_interv_success }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.36.d_ko
				trigger = { always = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = ca_central_america.36.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_military_parade" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		exists = c:HON
	}

	immediate = {
		remove_variable = frca_interv_success
		c:HON = { save_scope_as = ca_target }
		if = {
			limit = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes }
			set_variable = frca_interv_success
		}
		else = {
			random = { chance = ca_intervention_success_chance set_variable = frca_interv_success }
		}
	}

	# --- Exito ---
	# a. Dejar una guarnicion federal
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.36.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { has_variable = frca_interv_success }
		ca_frca_loyalty_change = { TAG = hon VALUE = 2 }
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_up = yes
		add_treasury = -5000
		set_variable = { name = frca_loyalty_min2_hon days = 365 }
		scope:ca_target = { add_modifier = { name = ca_federal_intervention_boost months = 12 } }
		set_global_variable = { name = hon_ferrera_out days = 730 }
		custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.36.ok_tt
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = { trigger = { gold_reserves < 5000 } add = -40 }
		}
	}
	# b. Retirar las tropas
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.36.b
		trigger = { has_variable = frca_interv_success }
		ca_frca_loyalty_change = { TAG = hon VALUE = 1 }
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_up = yes
		scope:ca_target = { add_modifier = { name = ca_federal_intervention_boost months = 12 } }
		set_global_variable = { name = hon_ferrera_out days = 730 }
		custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.36.ok_tt
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}

	# --- Fracaso ---
	# a. Retirarse
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.36.c
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { NOT = { has_variable = frca_interv_success } }
		ca_frca_loyalty_down = { TAG = hon }
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		set_global_variable = hon_ferrera_prestige
		ai_chance = { base = 60 }
	}
	# b. Enviar refuerzos: otra tirada; si vuelve a fallar, crisis y guerra civil del estado
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.36.e
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_variable = frca_interv_success }
			NOT = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes }
		}
		add_treasury = -10000
		random = { chance = ca_intervention_success_chance set_variable = frca_interv_reinforced }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = frca_interv_reinforced }
			remove_variable = frca_interv_reinforced
			custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.reinforce_ok_tt
			ca_frca_loyalty_change = { TAG = hon VALUE = 1 }
			ca_frca_bar_moderate_up = yes
			scope:ca_target = { add_modifier = { name = ca_federal_intervention_boost months = 12 } }
		}
		else = {
			custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.reinforce_ko_tt
			ca_frca_loyalty_down = { TAG = hon }
			ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
			scope:ca_target = { set_variable = opposition_crisis_var }
		set_global_variable = hon_ferrera_prestige
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = { trigger = { gold_reserves < 10000 } add = -40 }
		}
	}

		default_option = yes
		trigger = { has_variable = frca_interv_success }
		ca_frca_loyalty_change = { TAG = els VALUE = 2 }
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_up = yes
		add_treasury = -5000
		set_variable = { name = frca_loyalty_min2_els days = 365 }
		scope:ca_target = { add_modifier = { name = ca_federal_intervention_boost months = 12 } }
		set_variable = { name = frca_loyalty_min2_els days = 730 }
		custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.38.ok_tt
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = { trigger = { gold_reserves < 5000 } add = -40 }
		}
	}
	# b. Retirar las tropas
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.38.b
		trigger = { has_variable = frca_interv_success }
		ca_frca_loyalty_change = { TAG = els VALUE = 1 }
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_up = yes
		scope:ca_target = { add_modifier = { name = ca_federal_intervention_boost months = 12 } }
		set_variable = { name = frca_loyalty_min2_els days = 730 }
		custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.38.ok_tt
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}

	# --- Fracaso ---
	# a. Retirarse
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.38.c
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { NOT = { has_variable = frca_interv_success } }
		ca_frca_loyalty_down = { TAG = els }
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes

		ai_chance = { base = 60 }
	}
	# b. Enviar refuerzos: otra tirada; si vuelve a fallar, crisis y guerra civil del estado
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.38.e
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_variable = frca_interv_success }
			NOT = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes }
		}
		add_treasury = -10000
		random = { chance = ca_intervention_success_chance set_variable = frca_interv_reinforced }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = frca_interv_reinforced }
			remove_variable = frca_interv_reinforced
			custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.reinforce_ok_tt
			ca_frca_loyalty_change = { TAG = els VALUE = 1 }
			ca_frca_bar_moderate_up = yes
			scope:ca_target = { add_modifier = { name = ca_federal_intervention_boost months = 12 } }
		}
		else = {
			custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.reinforce_ko_tt
			ca_frca_loyalty_down = { TAG = els }
			ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
			scope:ca_target = { set_variable = opposition_crisis_var }

		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = { trigger = { gold_reserves < 10000 } add = -40 }
		}
	}
}

## UCA Accept the intervention in Guatemala 
# CAFM F7 (grupo 7): resultado de la intervencion federal en Guatemala.
# Exito: 50 % + 10 % por nivel de lealtad +- ejercito y ajuste del estado (ca_intervention_success_chance).
# Con la IA historica se fuerza el resultado real.
ca_central_america.39 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.39.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.39.d_ok
				trigger = { has_variable = frca_interv_success }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.39.d_ko
				trigger = { always = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = ca_central_america.39.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_military_parade" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		exists = c:GUA
	}

	immediate = {
		remove_variable = frca_interv_success
		c:GUA = { save_scope_as = ca_target }
		# Historico: antes de 1840, exito parcial (Carrera vuelve en 12 meses); desde 1840, fracaso
		if = {
			limit = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { game_date < 1840.1.1 }
				set_variable = frca_interv_success
				set_variable = { name = frca_gua_carrera_returns days = 365 }
				set_variable = frca_gua_partial_success
			}
		}
		else = {
			random = { chance = ca_intervention_success_chance set_variable = frca_interv_success }
		}
	}

	# --- Exito ---
	# a. Dejar una guarnicion federal
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.39.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { has_variable = frca_interv_success }
		ca_frca_loyalty_change = { TAG = gua VALUE = 2 }
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_up = yes
		add_treasury = -5000
		set_variable = { name = frca_loyalty_min2_gua days = 365 }
		scope:ca_target = { add_modifier = { name = ca_federal_intervention_boost months = 12 } }
		custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.39.ok_tt
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = { trigger = { gold_reserves < 5000 } add = -40 }
		}
	}
	# b. Retirar las tropas
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.39.b
		trigger = { has_variable = frca_interv_success }
		ca_frca_loyalty_change = { TAG = gua VALUE = 1 }
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_up = yes
		scope:ca_target = { add_modifier = { name = ca_federal_intervention_boost months = 12 } }
		custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.39.ok_tt
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}

	# --- Fracaso ---
	# a. Retirarse
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.39.c
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { NOT = { has_variable = frca_interv_success } }
		ca_frca_loyalty_down = { TAG = gua }
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		set_variable = frca_carrera_strengthened
		set_global_variable = gua_carrera_power
		ai_chance = { base = 60 }
	}
	# b. Enviar refuerzos: otra tirada; si vuelve a fallar, crisis y guerra civil del estado
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.39.e
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_variable = frca_interv_success }
			NOT = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes }
		}
		add_treasury = -10000
		random = { chance = ca_intervention_success_chance set_variable = frca_interv_reinforced }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = frca_interv_reinforced }
			remove_variable = frca_interv_reinforced
			custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.reinforce_ok_tt
			ca_frca_loyalty_change = { TAG = gua VALUE = 1 }
			ca_frca_bar_moderate_up = yes
			scope:ca_target = { add_modifier = { name = ca_federal_intervention_boost months = 12 } }
		}
		else = {
			custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.reinforce_ko_tt
			ca_frca_loyalty_down = { TAG = gua }
			ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
			scope:ca_target = { set_variable = opposition_crisis_var }
		set_variable = frca_carrera_strengthened
		set_global_variable = gua_carrera_power
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = { trigger = { gold_reserves < 10000 } add = -40 }
		}
	}
}

## UCA Accept the intervention in Los Altos 
# CAFM F7 (grupo 7): resultado de la intervencion federal en Los Altos.

# Con la IA historica se fuerza el resultado real.
ca_central_america.40 = {

				desc = ca_central_america.40.d_ok

			}

				desc = ca_central_america.40.d_ko

#UCA Do not accept the intervention (Genérico)
# CAFM F7 (grupo 7): intervencion rechazada. 180 dias sin intervenir; +5 % de radicales en el
# ejercito; sin barra. Consecuencia segun el estado (la b la evita con un 50 %).
ca_central_america.41 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.41.t
	desc = ca_central_america.41.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.41.f
	event_image = { video = "africa_diplomats_negotiating" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_diplomacy.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		has_variable = frca_refused_target
	}

	immediate = {
		var:frca_refused_target = { save_scope_as = ca_target }
		remove_variable = frca_refused_target
		set_variable = { name = ca_intervention_cooldown_var days = 180 }
		add_radicals = { value = 0.05 interest_group = ig:ig_armed_forces }
	}

	# a. Es lo mejor
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.41.a
		default_option = yes
		ca_frca_refusal_consequence = yes
		ai_chance = { base = 70 }
	}
	# b. Enviar un emisario: reduce a la mitad la consecuencia
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.41.b
		add_treasury = { value = ca_envoy_cost multiply = -1 }
		custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.41.b_tt
		random = {
			chance = 50
			ca_frca_refusal_consequence = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
}
## Historic Second Central American Civil War/ End of the Union?
# CAFM F8 (grupo 8): la guerra federal (fase III). Trigger: fase III y barra <= 50 o 2 o mas
# estados nucleo separados; con la IA historica, en 1839. No independiza a nadie.
ca_central_america.42 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.42.t
	desc = ca_central_america.42.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.42.f
	event_image = { video = "europenorthamerica_before_the_battle" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		ca_frca_journal_active = yes
		NOT = { has_variable = frca_fw_event_fired }
		ca_frca_phase_ge = { VALUE = 3 }
		NOT = { ca_frca_phase_ge = { VALUE = 4 } }
		ca_core_states_independent_count >= 1
		is_at_war = no
		OR = {
			AND = {
				ca_frca_ai_historic = yes
				game_date >= 1839.3.1
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes }
				OR = {
					je:je_historic_central_america_falls_apart ?= { "scripted_bar_progress(frca_support_bar)" <= ca_frca_bar_crisis }
					ca_core_states_separated_count >= 2
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = frca_fw_event_fired
	}

	# a. Luchar: una guerra de reanexion contra cada estado nucleo independiente
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.42.a
		default_option = yes
		set_variable = frca_federal_war_declared
		set_variable = { name = frca_fw_fresh days = 30 }
		custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.42.a_tt
		ca_frca_federal_war_target = { TAG = gua CTAG = GUA STATE = STATE_GUATEMALA }
		ca_frca_federal_war_target = { TAG = hon CTAG = HON STATE = STATE_HONDURAS }
		ca_frca_federal_war_target = { TAG = nic CTAG = NIC STATE = STATE_NICARAGUA }
		ca_frca_federal_war_target = { TAG = cos CTAG = COS STATE = STATE_COSTA_RICA }
		ca_frca_federal_war_target = { TAG = els CTAG = ELS STATE = STATE_SAN_SALVADOR }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
		}
	}

	# b. Aceptar la disolucion: el diario fracasa (D1)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.42.b
		set_variable = frca_lost_war
		custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.42.b_tt
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				trigger = { je:je_historic_central_america_falls_apart ?= { "scripted_bar_progress(frca_support_bar)" < 30 } }
				add = 40
			}
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -1000 }
		}
	}
}
## Defeat in the Second Civil War
# CAFM F8 (grupo 8): derrota en la guerra federal. Solo pone frca_lost_war: el diario fracasa
# (D1, .13). Ya no cambia la etiqueta ni exilia a nadie.
ca_central_america.43 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.43.t
	desc = ca_central_america.43.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.43.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_devastation" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/bicorne_hat.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		has_variable = frca_fw_resolved
		NOT = { has_variable = frca_lost_war }
		NOT = { has_variable = frca_won_war }
	}

	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.43.a
		default_option = yes
		set_variable = frca_lost_war
	}
}

## Victory in the Second Civil War
# CAFM F8 (grupo 8): victoria en la guerra federal. Ningun estado nucleo independiente: pone
# frca_won_war, pasa a la fase IV y abre el desenlace B.
ca_central_america.44 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.44.t
	desc = ca_central_america.44.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.44.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_military_parade" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/bicorne_hat.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		has_variable = frca_fw_resolved
		NOT = { has_variable = frca_won_war }
		NOT = { has_variable = frca_lost_war }
		ca_core_states_independent_count = 0
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = frca_won_war
		ca_frca_set_phase = { VALUE = 4 }
	}

	# a. Viva la Federacion!
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.44.a
		default_option = yes
		add_modifier = { name = ca_frca_federal_victory months = 60 }
		ai_chance = { base = 70 }
	}
	# b. Clemencia: lealtad +1 a los reanexados; -autoridad
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.44.b
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = frca_fw_target_nic }
			ca_frca_loyalty_change = { TAG = nic VALUE = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = frca_fw_target_hon }
			ca_frca_loyalty_change = { TAG = hon VALUE = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = frca_fw_target_cos }
			ca_frca_loyalty_change = { TAG = cos VALUE = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = frca_fw_target_gua }
			ca_frca_loyalty_change = { TAG = gua VALUE = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = frca_fw_target_els }
			ca_frca_loyalty_change = { TAG = els VALUE = 1 }
		}
		add_modifier = { name = ca_frca_clemency months = 24 }
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
}

## Costa Rica declares independence 15 November de 1838
# CAFM F6 (grupo 11): respuesta de la UCA a la independencia de Costa Rica (paso 2).
# Historico (IA 80 %): 1838.11.15. Dinamico: peticion pacifica del estado (.26).
ca_central_america.45 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.45.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.45.d_petition
				trigger = { has_variable = frca_cos_by_petition }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.45.d
				trigger = { always = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = ca_central_america.45.f
	event_image = { video = "gfx/event_pictures/southamerica_aristocrats.bk2" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_map.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		NOT = { has_variable = frca_cos_independence_done }
		c:COS ?= {
			is_subject_of = c:UCA
			is_subject_type = subject_type_ca_dependency
		}
		ca_frca_loyalty_le = { TAG = cos VALUE = 0 }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				ca_frca_ai_historic = yes
				game_date >= 1838.11.15
			}
			has_variable = frca_cos_independence_request
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = frca_cos_independence_done
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = frca_cos_independence_request }
			set_variable = { name = frca_cos_by_petition days = 30 }
		}
		remove_variable = frca_cos_independence_request
		c:COS = { save_scope_as = secession_country }
	}

	# a. Reconocer la independencia
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.45.a
		default_option = yes
		ca_frca_recognize_independence = { TAG = cos CTAG = COS NAME = costarica RELATIONS = 20 }
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -40 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
		}
	}
	# b. Negar: el estado elige someterse o alzarse en armas
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.45.b
		s:STATE_COSTA_RICA ?= { add_claim = c:UCA }
		scope:secession_country = {
			change_relations = { country = c:UCA value = -50 }
			set_variable = ca_independence_denied
			trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.26 days = 7 }
		}
		remove_variable = frca_cos_independence_done
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 40 }
		}
	}
}

## Nicaragua declares independence 30 de abril de 1838
# CAFM F6 (grupo 11): respuesta de la UCA a la independencia de Nicaragua (paso 2).
# Historico (IA 80 %): 1838.4.30. Dinamico: peticion pacifica del estado (.27).
ca_central_america.46 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.46.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.46.d_petition
				trigger = { has_variable = frca_nic_by_petition }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.46.d
				trigger = { always = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = ca_central_america.46.f
	event_image = { video = "gfx/event_pictures/southamerica_aristocrats.bk2" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_map.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		NOT = { has_variable = frca_nic_independence_done }
		c:NIC ?= {
			is_subject_of = c:UCA
			is_subject_type = subject_type_ca_dependency
		}
		ca_frca_loyalty_le = { TAG = nic VALUE = 0 }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				ca_frca_ai_historic = yes
				game_date >= 1838.4.30
			}
			has_variable = frca_nic_independence_request
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = frca_nic_independence_done
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = frca_nic_independence_request }
			set_variable = { name = frca_nic_by_petition days = 30 }
		}
		remove_variable = frca_nic_independence_request
		c:NIC = { save_scope_as = secession_country }
	}

	# a. Reconocer la independencia
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.46.a
		default_option = yes
		ca_frca_recognize_independence = { TAG = nic CTAG = NIC NAME = nicaragua RELATIONS = 20 }
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -40 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
		}
	}
	# b. Negar: el estado elige someterse o alzarse en armas
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.46.b
		s:STATE_NICARAGUA ?= { add_claim = c:UCA }
		scope:secession_country = {
			change_relations = { country = c:UCA value = -50 }
			set_variable = ca_independence_denied
			trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.27 days = 7 }
		}
		remove_variable = frca_nic_independence_done
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 40 }
		}
	}
}

## Guatemala declares independence 17 de abril de 1839
# CAFM F6 (grupo 11): respuesta de la UCA a la independencia de Guatemala (paso 2).

## Honduras declares independence 5 de noviembre de 1839
# CAFM F6 (grupo 11): respuesta de la UCA a la independencia de Honduras (paso 2).
# Historico (IA 80 %): 1838.10.26. Dinamico: peticion pacifica del estado (.49).
ca_central_america.48 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.48.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.48.d_petition
				trigger = { has_variable = frca_hon_by_petition }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.48.d
				trigger = { always = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = ca_central_america.48.f
	event_image = { video = "gfx/event_pictures/southamerica_aristocrats.bk2" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_map.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		NOT = { has_variable = frca_hon_independence_done }
		c:HON ?= {
			is_subject_of = c:UCA
			is_subject_type = subject_type_ca_dependency
		}
		ca_frca_loyalty_le = { TAG = hon VALUE = 0 }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				ca_frca_ai_historic = yes
				game_date >= 1838.10.26
			}
			has_variable = frca_hon_independence_request
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = frca_hon_independence_done
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = frca_hon_independence_request }
			set_variable = { name = frca_hon_by_petition days = 30 }
		}
		remove_variable = frca_hon_independence_request
		c:HON = { save_scope_as = secession_country }
	}

	# a. Reconocer la independencia
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.48.a
		default_option = yes

		ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -40 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
		}
	}
	# b. Negar: el estado elige someterse o alzarse en armas

			base = 40

		}
	}

## Honduras declares independence Peaceful
# CAFM F6 (grupo 11): declaracion de independencia de Honduras (lado del estado, solo dinamico).
# Tras *Falls* (subdito ca_dependency con lealtad 0). Tambien vuelve si la UCA niega la peticion.
ca_central_america.49 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.49.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.49.d_denied
				trigger = { has_variable = ca_independence_denied }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.49.d
				trigger = { always = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = ca_central_america.49.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_signed_contract" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		c:HON ?= this
		is_subject_of = c:UCA
		is_subject_type = subject_type_ca_dependency
		ca_frca_loyalty_le = { TAG = hon VALUE = 0 }
		NOT = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes }
		is_at_war = no
		OR = {
			has_variable = ca_independence_denied
			AND = {
				ca_frca_phase_ge = { VALUE = 2 }
				NOT = { ca_frca_phase_ge = { VALUE = 4 } }
				NOT = { has_variable = ca_independence_declared }
				NOT = { has_variable = ca_declaration_wait }
				NOT = { has_journal_entry = je_ca_federalists_vs_separatists_situation }
				NOT = { has_variable = ca_fvs_result_beats }
			}
		}
	}

	# a. Declararla pacificamente: peticion a la UCA
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.49.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { NOT = { has_variable = ca_independence_denied } }
		set_variable = ca_independence_declared
		c:UCA ?= {
			set_variable = frca_hon_independence_request
			trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.48 days = 7 popup = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
	# b. Declararla en armas
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.49.b
		set_variable = ca_independence_declared
		remove_variable = ca_independence_denied
		c:UCA ?= {
			set_variable = frca_hon_armed_pending
			trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.68 days = 7 popup = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { is_powerful = yes } } add = 20 }
		}
	}
	# c. Esperar: vuelve en 6 meses
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.49.c
		trigger = { NOT = { has_variable = ca_independence_denied } }
		set_variable = { name = ca_declaration_wait days = 180 }
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
	# d. Someterse (tras la negativa de la UCA)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.49.d_opt
		trigger = { has_variable = ca_independence_denied }
		remove_variable = ca_independence_denied
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = hon VALUE = 1 }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { is_powerful = yes } } add = -20 }
		}
	}
}

## El Salvador Beats to secessionists
# CAFM F6 (grupo 9): *Beats* en El Salvador, ganan los federalistas. Lo lanza ca_fvs_resolve.
ca_central_america.50 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.50.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_fvs_beats_election_desc
				trigger = { scope:secession_country = { has_variable = ca_fvs_cause_election } }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_fvs_beats_coup_desc
				trigger = { scope:secession_country = { has_variable = ca_fvs_cause_coup } }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_fvs_beats_civil_war_desc
				trigger = { scope:secession_country = { has_variable = ca_fvs_cause_civil_war } }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_fvs_beats_morazan_desc
				trigger = { scope:secession_country = { has_variable = ca_fvs_cause_morazan } }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.50.d
				trigger = { always = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = ca_central_america.50.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_military_parade" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		c:ELS ?= { has_variable = ca_fvs_result_beats }
	}

	immediate = {
		c:ELS = { save_scope_as = secession_country }
	}

	# a. Reincorporarlo como provincia
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.50.a
		annex = scope:secession_country
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = els VALUE = 3 }
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_up = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 50 }
		}
	}
	# b. Mantenerlo como estado miembro
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.50.b
		default_option = yes
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = els VALUE = 3 }
		scope:secession_country = {
			add_modifier = { name = ca_federal_loyalty_restored months = 60 }
		}
		ca_frca_bar_minor_up = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -50 }
		}
	}
}

## El Salvador falls to secessionists
# CAFM F6 (grupo 9): *Falls* en El Salvador, ganan los separatistas. Lo lanza ca_fvs_resolve.
ca_central_america.51 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.51.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_fvs_falls_election_desc
				trigger = { scope:secession_country = { has_variable = ca_fvs_cause_election } }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_fvs_falls_coup_desc
				trigger = { scope:secession_country = { has_variable = ca_fvs_cause_coup } }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_fvs_falls_civil_war_desc
				trigger = { scope:secession_country = { has_variable = ca_fvs_cause_civil_war } }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_fvs_falls_assembly_desc
				trigger = { scope:secession_country = { has_variable = ca_fvs_cause_assembly } }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_fvs_falls_revolt_desc
				trigger = { scope:secession_country = { has_variable = ca_fvs_cause_revolt } }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.51.d
				trigger = { always = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = ca_central_america.51.f
	event_image = { video = "southamerica_war_civilians" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		c:ELS ?= { has_variable = ca_fvs_result_falls }
	}

	immediate = {
		c:ELS = { save_scope_as = secession_country }
	}

	# a. Aceptar: el estado sigue al paso 2
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.51.a
		default_option = yes
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = els VALUE = 0 }
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -40 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
		}
	}
	# b. Preparar la reconquista (cuenta para la guerra federal .42)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.51.b
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = els VALUE = 0 }
		s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR ?= { add_claim = c:UCA }
		set_variable = frca_reconquest_els
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 40 }
		}
	}
}

## El Salvador declares independence Peaceful
# CAFM F6 (grupo 11): declaracion de independencia de El Salvador (lado del estado, solo dinamico).
# Tras *Falls* (subdito ca_dependency con lealtad 0). Tambien vuelve si la UCA niega la peticion.
ca_central_america.52 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.52.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.52.d_denied
				trigger = { has_variable = ca_independence_denied }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.52.d
				trigger = { always = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = ca_central_america.52.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_signed_contract" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		c:ELS ?= this
		is_subject_of = c:UCA
		is_subject_type = subject_type_ca_dependency
		ca_frca_loyalty_le = { TAG = els VALUE = 0 }
		NOT = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes }
		is_at_war = no
		# El Salvador solo se separa si la capital de la UCA no esta en San Salvador (grupo 11)
		c:UCA ?= { capital = { NOT = { state_region = s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR } } }
		OR = {
			has_variable = ca_independence_denied
			AND = {
				ca_frca_phase_ge = { VALUE = 2 }
				NOT = { has_variable = ca_independence_declared }
				NOT = { has_variable = ca_declaration_wait }
				NOT = { has_journal_entry = je_ca_federalists_vs_separatists_situation }
				NOT = { has_variable = ca_fvs_result_beats }
			}
		}
	}

	# a. Declararla pacificamente: peticion a la UCA
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.52.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { NOT = { has_variable = ca_independence_denied } }
		set_variable = ca_independence_declared
		c:UCA ?= {
			set_variable = frca_els_independence_request
			trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.99 days = 7 popup = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
	# b. Declararla en armas
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.52.b
		set_variable = ca_independence_declared
		remove_variable = ca_independence_denied
		c:UCA ?= {
			set_variable = frca_els_armed_pending
			trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.72 days = 7 popup = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { is_powerful = yes } } add = 20 }
		}
	}
	# c. Esperar: vuelve en 6 meses
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.52.c
		trigger = { NOT = { has_variable = ca_independence_denied } }
		set_variable = { name = ca_declaration_wait days = 180 }
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
	# d. Someterse (tras la negativa de la UCA)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.52.d_opt
		trigger = { has_variable = ca_independence_denied }
		remove_variable = ca_independence_denied
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = els VALUE = 1 }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { is_powerful = yes } } add = -20 }
		}
	}
}

		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = gua VALUE = 3 }
		scope:secession_country = {
			add_modifier = { name = ca_federal_loyalty_restored months = 60 }
		}
		ca_frca_bar_minor_up = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -50 }
		}
	}
}

		}
	}

## Guatemala declares independence Peaceful
# CAFM F6 (grupo 11): declaracion de independencia de Guatemala (lado del estado, solo dinamico).
# Tras *Falls* (subdito ca_dependency con lealtad 0). Tambien vuelve si la UCA niega la peticion.
ca_central_america.55 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.55.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.55.d_denied
				trigger = { has_variable = ca_independence_denied }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.55.d
				trigger = { always = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = ca_central_america.55.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_signed_contract" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		c:GUA ?= this
		is_subject_of = c:UCA
		is_subject_type = subject_type_ca_dependency
		ca_frca_loyalty_le = { TAG = gua VALUE = 0 }
		NOT = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes }
		is_at_war = no
		OR = {
			has_variable = ca_independence_denied
			AND = {
				ca_frca_phase_ge = { VALUE = 2 }
				NOT = { ca_frca_phase_ge = { VALUE = 4 } }
				NOT = { has_variable = ca_independence_declared }
				NOT = { has_variable = ca_declaration_wait }
				NOT = { has_journal_entry = je_ca_federalists_vs_separatists_situation }
				NOT = { has_variable = ca_fvs_result_beats }
			}
		}
	}

	# a. Declararla pacificamente: peticion a la UCA
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.55.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { NOT = { has_variable = ca_independence_denied } }
		set_variable = ca_independence_declared
		c:UCA ?= {
			set_variable = frca_gua_independence_request
			trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.47 days = 7 popup = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
	# b. Declararla en armas
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.55.b
		set_variable = ca_independence_declared
		remove_variable = ca_independence_denied
		c:UCA ?= {
			set_variable = frca_gua_armed_pending
			trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.70 days = 7 popup = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { is_powerful = yes } } add = 20 }
		}
	}
	# c. Esperar: vuelve en 6 meses
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.55.c
		trigger = { NOT = { has_variable = ca_independence_denied } }
		set_variable = { name = ca_declaration_wait days = 180 }
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
	# d. Someterse (tras la negativa de la UCA)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.55.d_opt
		trigger = { has_variable = ca_independence_denied }
		remove_variable = ca_independence_denied
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = gua VALUE = 1 }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { is_powerful = yes } } add = -20 }
		}
	}
}

			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_fvs_beats_civil_war_desc
				trigger = { scope:secession_country = { has_variable = ca_fvs_cause_civil_war } }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_fvs_beats_morazan_desc
				trigger = { scope:secession_country = { has_variable = ca_fvs_cause_morazan } }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.56.d
				trigger = { always = yes }
			}
		}

	flavor = ca_central_america.56.f

	# a. Reincorporarlo como provincia

		name = ca_central_america.56.a
		set_variable = frca_alt_annexed
		annex = scope:secession_country
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = alt VALUE = 3 }
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_up = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 50 }
		}
	}
	# b. Mantenerlo como estado miembro
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.56.b
		default_option = yes
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = alt VALUE = 3 }
		scope:secession_country = {
			add_modifier = { name = ca_federal_loyalty_restored months = 60 }
		}
		ca_frca_bar_minor_up = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -50 }
		}
	}
}

# CAFM F14: reescrito
ca_central_america.59 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.59.t
	desc = ca_central_america.59.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.59.f
	event_image = { video = "africa_diplomats_negotiating" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_diplomacy.dds"
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		c:PNM ?= this # arreglo 10 nov: el Istmo puede no existir
		is_subject = no
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_isthmus_59_fired }
		has_global_variable = ca_pnm_born
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_pnm_young }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.59.a
		default_option = yes
		set_variable = ca_isthmus_59_fired
		add_modifier = { name = ca_isthmus_neutral_transit months = 120 }
		ai_chance = { base = 60 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.59.b
		set_variable = ca_isthmus_59_fired
		if = {
			limit = { exists = c:USA }
			change_relations = { country = c:USA value = 50 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { exists = c:GBR }
			change_relations = { country = c:GBR value = 50 }
		}
		set_variable = ca_pnm_seeks_protector
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
}

## Panama declares independence Peaceful
# CAFM F14: reescrito
ca_central_america.61 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.61.t
	desc = ca_central_america.61.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.61.f
	event_image = { video = "southamerica_war_civilians" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:CLM
		NOT = { has_global_variable = ca_pnm_born }
		NOT = { exists = c:PNM }
		any_scope_state = { state_region = s:STATE_PANAMA }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				has_global_variable = ca_world_historic
				game_date >= 1840.11.18
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { has_global_variable = ca_world_historic }
				game_date >= 1839.1.1
				OR = {
					is_at_war = yes
					any_scope_state = {
						state_region = s:STATE_PANAMA
						turmoil > ca_loyalty_turmoil_threshold
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_global_variable = ca_pnm_born
		create_country = {
			tag = PNM
			origin = root
			state = s:STATE_PANAMA.region_state:CLM
		}
		ca_isthmus_ask_recognition = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.61.a
		default_option = yes
		set_variable = { name = ca_pnm_negotiating days = 365 }
		set_global_variable = ca_pnm_negotiating
		ai_chance = { base = 80 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.61.b
		create_diplomatic_play = {
			name = ca_isthmus_war_play
			target_country = c:PNM
			type = dp_annex_war
		}
		c:PNM ?= { trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.75 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = { base = 20
		modifier = { trigger = { has_global_variable = ca_world_historic } factor = 0 } }
	}
}

## UCA's Panama declares independence Peaceful
# CAFM F14: reescrito
