	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_diplomacy.dds"
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_isthmus_62_fired }
		NOT = { has_global_variable = ca_world_historic }
		c:PNM ?= {
			is_subject = no
			"relations(c:UCA)" >= relations_threshold:friendly
		}
		has_global_variable = ca_pnm_supported
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.62.a
		default_option = yes
		set_variable = ca_isthmus_62_fired
		create_diplomatic_pact = {
			country = c:PNM
			type = ca_dependency
		}
		set_variable = { name = frca_loyalty_pnm value = 2 }
		ca_frca_bar_minor_up = yes
		c:CLM ?= {
			change_relations = { country = root value = -50 }
			s:STATE_PANAMA = { add_claim = c:CLM }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 60 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.62.b
		set_variable = ca_isthmus_62_fired
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
}

## Civil war in Nicaragua
# CAFM F7 (grupo 10): guerra civil en Nicaragua (lado del estado). Puerta: la marca de crisis
# (.24, .25 b, intervencion fallida) o, con la IA historica, la fecha real. Fases I-III, sin guerra,
# una vez. Abre el capitulo de la UCA (.101 a los 7 dias, .102 a los 60; resolucion .103 a los
# 180) y la resolucion llama a ca_fvs_resolve (causa: guerra civil).
ca_central_america.63 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.63.t
	desc = ca_central_america.63.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.63.f
	event_image = { video = "southamerica_war_civilians" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		c:NIC ?= this
		is_subject_of = c:UCA
		is_at_war = no
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_cw_fired }
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_no_civil_war }
		NOT = { ca_frca_phase_ge = { VALUE = 4 } }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				has_variable = opposition_crisis_var
				ca_frca_phase_ge = { VALUE = 2 }
			}
			AND = {
				ca_frca_ai_historic = yes
				game_date >= 1837.1.1
				game_date < 1838.4.30
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = ca_cw_fired
		remove_variable = opposition_crisis_var
		if = {
			limit = {
				ca_frca_ai_historic = yes
				game_date >= 1837.1.1
				game_date < 1838.4.30
			}
			set_variable = ca_cw_winner_separatists
		}
		c:UCA ?= { ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes }
	}

	# a. Combatir a los rebeldes (historica): +20 % al bando del gobierno
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.63.a
		default_option = yes
		if = {
			# El gobierno es federalista si la barra del estado esta en 50 o mas (o si no tiene barra)
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = ca_fvs_bar }
					var:ca_fvs_bar >= 50
				}
			}
			ca_cw_help_change = { VALUE = 2 }
		}
		else = {
			ca_cw_help_change = { VALUE = -2 }
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			add_modifier = { name = state_separatist_unrest months = 12 }
		}
		ca_cw_start_chapter = { TAG = nic A = 101 B = 102 }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { is_powerful = yes } } add = 20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
		}
	}
	# b. Pedir la intervencion federal
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.63.b
		set_variable = { name = ca_intervention_request_active days = 180 }
		ca_cw_start_chapter = { TAG = nic A = 101 B = 102 }
		c:UCA ?= { trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.28 days = 1 popup = yes } }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_loyalty_ge = { TAG = nic VALUE = 2 } } add = 20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -25 }
		}
	}
	# c. Negociar: sin guerra; lealtad -1; los separatistas ganan influencia
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.63.c
		ca_frca_loyalty_change = { TAG = nic VALUE = -1 }
		ca_fvs_bar_change = { VALUE = -15 }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -25 }
		}
	}
}

#ca_central_america.64

## Civil war in Costa Rica
# CAFM F7 (grupo 10): guerra civil en Costa Rica (lado del estado). Puerta: la marca de crisis
# (.24, .25 b, intervencion fallida) o, con la IA historica, la fecha real. Fases I-III, sin guerra,
# una vez. Abre el capitulo de la UCA (.104 a los 7 dias, .105 a los 60; resolucion .106 a los
# 180) y la resolucion llama a ca_fvs_resolve (causa: guerra civil).
ca_central_america.65 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.65.t
	desc = ca_central_america.65.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.65.f
	event_image = { video = "southamerica_war_civilians" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		c:COS ?= this
		is_subject_of = c:UCA
		is_at_war = no
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_cw_fired }
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_no_civil_war }
		NOT = { ca_frca_phase_ge = { VALUE = 4 } }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				has_variable = opposition_crisis_var
				ca_frca_phase_ge = { VALUE = 2 }
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = ca_cw_fired
		remove_variable = opposition_crisis_var
		c:UCA ?= { ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes }
	}

	# a. Combatir a los rebeldes (historica): +20 % al bando del gobierno
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.65.a
		default_option = yes
		if = {
			# El gobierno es federalista si la barra del estado esta en 50 o mas (o si no tiene barra)
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = ca_fvs_bar }
					var:ca_fvs_bar >= 50
				}
			}
			ca_cw_help_change = { VALUE = 2 }
		}
		else = {
			ca_cw_help_change = { VALUE = -2 }
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			add_modifier = { name = state_separatist_unrest months = 12 }
		}
		ca_cw_start_chapter = { TAG = cos A = 104 B = 105 }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { is_powerful = yes } } add = 20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
		}
	}
	# b. Pedir la intervencion federal
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.65.b
		set_variable = { name = ca_intervention_request_active days = 180 }
		ca_cw_start_chapter = { TAG = cos A = 104 B = 105 }
		c:UCA ?= { trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.29 days = 1 popup = yes } }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_loyalty_ge = { TAG = cos VALUE = 2 } } add = 20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -25 }
		}
	}
	# c. Negociar: sin guerra; lealtad -1; los separatistas ganan influencia
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.65.c
		ca_frca_loyalty_change = { TAG = cos VALUE = -1 }
		ca_fvs_bar_change = { VALUE = -15 }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -25 }
		}
	}
}

## Costa Rica declares independence on Arms
# CAFM F6 (grupo 12): levantamiento armado en Costa Rica. Solo camino dinamico: llega desde la
# declaracion en armas del estado, la negativa de la UCA o la mano dura del paso 1.
ca_central_america.66 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.66.t
	desc = ca_central_america.66.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.66.f
	event_image = { video = "southamerica_war_civilians" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		has_variable = frca_cos_armed_pending
		c:COS ?= { is_subject_of = c:UCA }
	}

	immediate = {
		remove_variable = frca_cos_armed_pending
		c:COS = { save_scope_as = secession_country }
	}

	# a. Aplastar el levantamiento: guerra de independencia
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.66.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:secession_country = {
			create_diplomatic_play = {
				target_country = c:UCA
				type = dp_independence
			}
		}
		set_variable = frca_indep_war_cos
		set_variable = { name = frca_indep_war_grace_cos days = 90 }
		custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.armed_war_tt
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 30 }
		}
	}
	# b. Dejarlos ir
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.66.b
		ca_frca_recognize_independence = { TAG = cos CTAG = COS NAME = costarica RELATIONS = -20 }
		ca_frca_bar_major_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -10 }
		}
	}
	# c. Negociar: vuelve a ser miembro con lealtad 1 y concesiones
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.66.c
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = cos VALUE = 1 }
		scope:secession_country = {
			add_modifier = { name = ca_secession_concessions months = 24 }
			remove_variable = ca_independence_declared
		}
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -20 }
		}
	}
}

		}
		every_scope_state = {
			add_modifier = { name = state_separatist_unrest months = 12 }
		}
		ca_cw_start_chapter = { TAG = els A = 113 B = 114 }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { is_powerful = yes } } add = 20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
		}
	}
	# b. Pedir la intervencion federal
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.71.b
		set_variable = { name = ca_intervention_request_active days = 180 }
		ca_cw_start_chapter = { TAG = els A = 113 B = 114 }
		c:UCA ?= { trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.31 days = 1 popup = yes } }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_loyalty_ge = { TAG = els VALUE = 2 } } add = 20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -25 }
		}
	}
	# c. Negociar: sin guerra; lealtad -1; los separatistas ganan influencia
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.71.c
		ca_frca_loyalty_change = { TAG = els VALUE = -1 }
		ca_fvs_bar_change = { VALUE = -15 }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -25 }
		}
	}
}

## El Salvador declares independence on Arms
# CAFM F6 (grupo 12): levantamiento armado en El Salvador. Solo camino dinamico: llega desde la
# declaracion en armas del estado, la negativa de la UCA o la mano dura del paso 1.
ca_central_america.72 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.72.t
	desc = ca_central_america.72.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.72.f
	event_image = { video = "southamerica_war_civilians" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		has_variable = frca_els_armed_pending
		c:ELS ?= { is_subject_of = c:UCA }
		# El Salvador solo se separa si la capital de la UCA no esta en San Salvador (grupo 11)
		c:UCA ?= { capital = { NOT = { state_region = s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR } } }
	}

	immediate = {
		remove_variable = frca_els_armed_pending
		c:ELS = { save_scope_as = secession_country }
	}

	# a. Aplastar el levantamiento: guerra de independencia
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.72.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:secession_country = {
			create_diplomatic_play = {
				target_country = c:UCA
				type = dp_independence
			}
		}
		set_variable = frca_indep_war_els
		set_variable = { name = frca_indep_war_grace_els days = 90 }
		custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.armed_war_tt
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 30 }
		}
	}
	# b. Dejarlos ir
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.72.b
		ca_frca_recognize_independence = { TAG = els CTAG = ELS NAME = elsalvador RELATIONS = -20 }
		ca_frca_bar_major_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -10 }
		}
	}
	# c. Negociar: vuelve a ser miembro con lealtad 1 y concesiones
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.72.c
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = els VALUE = 1 }
		scope:secession_country = {
			add_modifier = { name = ca_secession_concessions months = 24 }
			remove_variable = ca_independence_declared
		}
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -20 }
		}
	}
}

## Civil war in Los Altos
# CAFM F7 (grupo 10): guerra civil en Los Altos (lado del estado). Puerta: la marca de crisis
# (.24, .25 b, intervencion fallida) o, con la IA historica, la fecha real. Fases I-III, sin guerra,
# una vez. Abre el capitulo de la UCA (.116 a los 7 dias, .117 a los 60; resolucion .118 a los
# 180) y la resolucion llama a ca_fvs_resolve (causa: guerra civil).
ca_central_america.73 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.73.t
	desc = ca_central_america.73.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.73.f
	event_image = { video = "southamerica_war_civilians" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		c:ALT ?= this
		is_subject_of = c:UCA
		is_at_war = no
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_cw_fired }
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_no_civil_war }
		NOT = { ca_frca_phase_ge = { VALUE = 4 } }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				has_variable = opposition_crisis_var
				ca_frca_phase_ge = { VALUE = 2 }
			}
			AND = {
				ca_frca_ai_historic = yes
				game_date >= 1840.1.1
				game_date < 1840.6.1
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = ca_cw_fired
		remove_variable = opposition_crisis_var
		if = {
			limit = {
				ca_frca_ai_historic = yes
				game_date >= 1840.1.1
				game_date < 1840.6.1
			}
			set_variable = ca_cw_winner_separatists
		}
		c:UCA ?= { ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes }
	}

	# a. Combatir a los rebeldes (historica): +20 % al bando del gobierno
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.73.a
		default_option = yes
		if = {
			# El gobierno es federalista si la barra del estado esta en 50 o mas (o si no tiene barra)
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = ca_fvs_bar }
					var:ca_fvs_bar >= 50
				}
			}
			ca_cw_help_change = { VALUE = 2 }
		}
		else = {
			ca_cw_help_change = { VALUE = -2 }
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			add_modifier = { name = state_separatist_unrest months = 12 }
		}
		ca_cw_start_chapter = { TAG = alt A = 116 B = 117 }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = { trigger = { ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { is_powerful = yes } } add = 20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
		}
	}
	# b. Pedir la intervencion federal
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.73.b
		set_variable = { name = ca_intervention_request_active days = 180 }
		ca_cw_start_chapter = { TAG = alt A = 116 B = 117 }
		c:UCA ?= { trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.33 days = 1 popup = yes } }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_loyalty_ge = { TAG = alt VALUE = 2 } } add = 20 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -25 }
		}
	}
	# c. Negociar: sin guerra; lealtad -1; los separatistas ganan influencia
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.73.c
		ca_frca_loyalty_change = { TAG = alt VALUE = -1 }
		ca_fvs_bar_change = { VALUE = -15 }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -25 }
		}
	}
}

## Los Altos declares independence on Arms
# CAFM F6 (grupo 12): levantamiento armado en Los Altos. Solo camino dinamico: llega desde la
# declaracion en armas del estado, la negativa de la UCA o la mano dura del paso 1.
ca_central_america.74 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.74.t
	desc = ca_central_america.74.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.74.f
	event_image = { video = "southamerica_war_civilians" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		has_variable = frca_alt_armed_pending
		c:ALT ?= { is_subject_of = c:UCA }
	}

	immediate = {
		remove_variable = frca_alt_armed_pending
		c:ALT = { save_scope_as = secession_country }
	}

	# a. Aplastar el levantamiento: guerra de independencia
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.74.a
		default_option = yes
		# Guatemala respalda a Los Altos si es separatista
		if = {
			limit = { c:GUA ?= { has_variable = ca_role_separatist } }
			scope:secession_country = {
				create_diplomatic_play = {
					target_country = c:UCA
					type = dp_independence
					add_initiator_backers = { c:GUA }
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			scope:secession_country = {
				create_diplomatic_play = {
					target_country = c:UCA
					type = dp_independence
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = frca_indep_war_alt
		set_variable = { name = frca_indep_war_grace_alt days = 90 }
		custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.armed_war_tt
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 30 }
		}
	}
	# b. Dejarlos ir
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.74.b
		ca_frca_recognize_independence = { TAG = alt CTAG = ALT NAME = losaltos RELATIONS = -20 }
		ca_frca_bar_major_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -10 }
		}
	}
	# c. Negociar: vuelve a ser miembro con lealtad 1 y concesiones
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.74.c
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = alt VALUE = 1 }
		scope:secession_country = {
			add_modifier = { name = ca_secession_concessions months = 24 }
			remove_variable = ca_independence_declared
		}
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -20 }
		}
	}
}

## Panama declares independence on Arms
# CAFM F14: reescrito
ca_central_america.76 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.76.t
	desc = ca_central_america.76.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.76.f
	event_image = { video = "southamerica_war_civilians" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:CLM
		NOT = { exists = c:PNM }
		NOT = { has_global_variable = ca_pnm_born }
		has_variable = ca_istmo_crisis
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.76.a
		default_option = yes
		remove_variable = ca_istmo_crisis
		ca_istmo_change = { VALUE = -40 }
		add_radicals = { value = small_radicals }
		s:STATE_PANAMA.region_state:CLM ?= { add_modifier = { name = ca_isthmus_occupation months = 36 } }
		ai_chance = { base = 70 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.76.b
		remove_variable = ca_istmo_crisis
		set_global_variable = ca_pnm_born
		create_country = {
			tag = PNM
			origin = root
			state = s:STATE_PANAMA.region_state:CLM
		}
		ca_isthmus_ask_recognition = yes
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
}


## Los Altos declares independence 31 de mayo de 1839
# CAFM F5 (grupos 11 y 14): respuesta de la UCA a la independencia de Los Altos miembro.
# Sin fecha fija (no hay fuente del 31 may 1839; en la historia Guatemala ocupa el altiplano
# el 29 ene 1840). Solo el camino dinamico: la peticion pacifica de Los Altos (.58, F6) pone
# frca_alt_independence_request en la UCA. No sale si Guatemala ya lo reabsorbio.
ca_central_america.78 = {
    type = country_event
    placement = root
    title = ca_central_america.78.t
    desc = ca_central_america.78.d
    flavor = ca_central_america.78.f
    duration = 3
    event_image = { video = "gfx/event_pictures/southamerica_aristocrats.bk2" }
    on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
    on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/southamerica/aristocrats"
    icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"

    trigger = {
        this = c:UCA
        has_variable = frca_alt_independence_request
        NOT = { has_variable = losaltos_independence_event_var }
        c:ALT ?= {
            is_subject_of = c:UCA
            is_subject_type = subject_type_ca_dependency
        }
        ca_frca_loyalty_le = { TAG = alt VALUE = 0 }
    }

    immediate = {
        set_variable = losaltos_independence_event_var
        remove_variable = frca_alt_independence_request
        c:ALT = {
            save_scope_as = secession_country
            capital = { save_scope_as = secession_capital }
        }
    }

    # a. Reconocer el Estado Libre de Los Altos
    option = {
        name = ca_central_america.78.a
        default_option = yes
        remove_diplomatic_pact = { country = scope:secession_country type = ca_dependency }
        scope:secession_country = {
            make_independent = yes
            set_variable = ca_independence_recognized
            change_relations = { country = ROOT value = 20 }
        }
        set_variable = frca_recognized_alt
        ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = alt VALUE = 0 }
        ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes
        ai_chance = {
            base = 60
            modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -40 }
            modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
        }
    }

    # b. Negar: es parte de Guatemala y de la Union. Los Altos elige someterse
    # o alzarse en armas (.74, F6).
    option = {
        name = ca_central_america.78.b
        set_variable = frca_alt_independence_denied
        scope:secession_country = {
            change_relations = { country = ROOT value = -50 }
            # CAFM F6: Los Altos elige someterse o alzarse en armas (.58 -> .74)
            set_variable = ca_independence_denied
            trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.58 days = 7 }
        }
        scope:secession_capital.state_region ?= { add_claim = ROOT }
        ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
        ai_chance = {
            base = 40
            modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 40 }
        }
    }
}

## Federal Congress authorizes states to reorganize themselves
# CAFM F6 (grupo 14): el decreto del 30 de mayo de 1838 = paso de la fase I a la II.
# IA: sale sola en la fecha. Jugador: boton "Convocar el Congreso" (desde la fecha) o solo a
# los 6 meses. Ruta centralista: solo b ("El gobierno disuelve el Congreso").
ca_central_america.79 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.79.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.79.d_centralist
				trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.79.d
				trigger = { always = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = ca_central_america.79.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_politicians_arguing" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		ca_frca_journal_active = yes
		game_date >= 1838.5.30
		NOT = { has_variable = frca_decree_event_fired }
		OR = {
			is_ai = yes
			has_variable = ca_self_determination
			game_date >= 1838.11.30
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = frca_decree_event_fired
	}

	# a. Permitir a los estados organizarse a su voluntad (federal, historica)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.79.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { ca_frca_route_federal = yes }
		ca_frca_decree_allow_effect = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
		}
	}

	# b. La Republica es una e indivisible (centralista; tambien en la federal)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.79.b
		set_variable = voting_ca_self_determination
		set_variable = frca_decree_vetoed
		if = {
			limit = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes }
			set_variable = frca_congress_dissolved
		}
		ca_frca_set_phase = { VALUE = 2 }
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_up = yes
		add_modifier = { name = unitary_stronghold }
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			add_modifier = { name = ca_ig_approval_strong_republic months = 24 }
		}
		ig:ig_landowners ?= {
			add_modifier = { name = ca_ig_disapproval_centralist_tyranny months = 24 }
		}
		ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= {
			add_modifier = { name = ca_federal_betrayal_disapproval months = 24 }
		}
		ruler ?= {
			add_modifier = { name = ca_popular_ruler_modifier months = 24 }
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_HONDURAS
					state_region = s:STATE_NICARAGUA
					state_region = s:STATE_COSTA_RICA
					state_region = s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR
				}
			}
			add_modifier = { name = ca_separatist_unrest months = 12 }
			add_devastation = 10
		}
		add_loyalists = { value = medium_radicals culture = cu:central_american }
		add_radicals = { value = medium_radicals culture = cu:guatemalan }
		add_radicals = { value = medium_radicals culture = cu:el_salvadorian }
		add_radicals = { value = medium_radicals culture = cu:honduran }
		add_radicals = { value = medium_radicals culture = cu:nicaraguan }
		add_radicals = { value = medium_radicals culture = cu:costa_rican }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 70 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = -30 }
		}
	}

	# c. Esta Union esta muerta (solo jugador, ruta federal): ruta separatista, abre .80
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.79.c
		trigger = {
			is_ai = no
			ca_frca_route_federal = yes
		}
		ca_frca_bar_major_down = yes
		set_variable = ca_faction_separatist
		set_variable = frca_selector_from_79
		trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.80 popup = yes }
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
}

# CAFM F4 (grupos 14 y 15): selector de estado, solo jugador. Dos contextos:
#   frca_selector_from_119: desde .119 c (1836, sin Los Altos). "Mantenerse leal" reabre .119.
#   frca_selector_from_79:  desde .79 c "Esta Union esta muerta" (F6). "Mantenerse leal"
#                           deshace la c y aplica los efectos de .79 a.
# Solo aparecen los estados que siguen en la UCA (provincias propias o subdito ca_dependency).
# El estado elegido recibe ca_role_separatist; la UCA pasa a la IA (federal, sorteo 80/20).
ca_central_america.80 = {
    type = country_event
    placement = root

    title = ca_central_america.80.t
    desc = ca_central_america.80.d
    flavor = ca_central_america.80.f

    event_image = { video = "southamerica_aristocrats" }
    icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

    trigger = {
        this = c:UCA
        is_ai = no
    }

    # Guatemala
    option = {
        name = ca_central_america.80.a
        trigger = {
            OR = {
                AND = {
                    NOT = { exists = c:GUA }
                    exists = s:STATE_GUATEMALA.region_state:UCA
                }
                c:GUA ?= { is_subject_of = c:UCA }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { exists = c:GUA } }
            ca_create_guatemala_effect = yes
        }
        ca_play_guatemala_effect = yes
        c:GUA ?= { set_variable = ca_role_separatist }
        # CAFM F6: en 1836 se crean solo los vecinos, como miembros en manos de la IA (rama 1)
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = frca_selector_from_119 }
            ca_create_neighbors_gua = yes
        }
        ca_frca_selector_leave_uca = yes
        play_as = c:GUA
        ai_chance = { base = 0 }
    }

    # El Salvador
    option = {
        name = ca_central_america.80.b
        trigger = {
            OR = {
                AND = {
                    NOT = { exists = c:ELS }
                    exists = s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR.region_state:UCA
                }
                c:ELS ?= { is_subject_of = c:UCA }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { exists = c:ELS } }
            ca_create_elsalvador_effect = yes
        }
        ca_play_elsalvador_effect = yes
        c:ELS ?= { set_variable = ca_role_separatist }
        # CAFM F6: en 1836 se crean solo los vecinos, como miembros en manos de la IA (rama 1)
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = frca_selector_from_119 }
            ca_create_neighbors_els = yes
        }
        ca_frca_selector_leave_uca = yes
        play_as = c:ELS
        ai_chance = { base = 0 }
    }

    # Honduras
    option = {
        name = ca_central_america.80.c
        trigger = {
            OR = {
                AND = {
                    NOT = { exists = c:HON }
                    exists = s:STATE_HONDURAS.region_state:UCA
                }
                c:HON ?= { is_subject_of = c:UCA }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { exists = c:HON } }
            ca_create_honduras_effect = yes
        }
        ca_play_honduras_effect = yes
        c:HON ?= { set_variable = ca_role_separatist }
        # CAFM F6: en 1836 se crean solo los vecinos, como miembros en manos de la IA (rama 1)
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = frca_selector_from_119 }
            ca_create_neighbors_hon = yes
        }
        ca_frca_selector_leave_uca = yes
        play_as = c:HON
        ai_chance = { base = 0 }
    }

    # Nicaragua
    option = {
        name = ca_central_america.80.nic
        trigger = {
            OR = {
                AND = {
                    NOT = { exists = c:NIC }
                    exists = s:STATE_NICARAGUA.region_state:UCA
                }
                c:NIC ?= { is_subject_of = c:UCA }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { exists = c:NIC } }
            ca_create_nicaragua_effect = yes
        }
        ca_play_nicaragua_effect = yes
        c:NIC ?= { set_variable = ca_role_separatist }
        # CAFM F6: en 1836 se crean solo los vecinos, como miembros en manos de la IA (rama 1)
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = frca_selector_from_119 }
            ca_create_neighbors_nic = yes
        }
        ca_frca_selector_leave_uca = yes
        play_as = c:NIC
        ai_chance = { base = 0 }
    }

    # Costa Rica
    option = {
        name = ca_central_america.80.e
        trigger = {
            OR = {
                AND = {
                    NOT = { exists = c:COS }
                    exists = s:STATE_COSTA_RICA.region_state:UCA
                }
                c:COS ?= { is_subject_of = c:UCA }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { exists = c:COS } }
            ca_create_costarica_effect = yes
        }
        ca_play_costarica_effect = yes
        c:COS ?= { set_variable = ca_role_separatist }
        # CAFM F6: en 1836 se crean solo los vecinos, como miembros en manos de la IA (rama 1)
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = frca_selector_from_119 }
            ca_create_neighbors_cos = yes
        }
        ca_frca_selector_leave_uca = yes
        play_as = c:COS
        ai_chance = { base = 0 }
    }

    # Los Altos: solo si la Federacion lo aprobo como estado miembro (.2 / .3).
    # Ya existe, con o sin Soconusco segun quien lo tenga: no se crea aqui.
    option = {
        name = ca_central_america.80.alt
        trigger = {
            NOT = { has_variable = frca_selector_from_119 }
            c:ALT ?= { is_subject_of = c:UCA }
        }
        ca_play_losaltos_effect = yes
        c:ALT ?= { set_variable = ca_role_separatist }
        ca_frca_selector_leave_uca = yes
        play_as = c:ALT
        ai_chance = { base = 0 }
    }

    # Mantenerse leal a la UCA
    option = {
        name = ca_central_america.80.g
        default_option = yes
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = frca_selector_from_79 }
            remove_variable = frca_selector_from_79
            remove_variable = ca_faction_separatist
            remove_variable = ca_state_selection_to_playing
            ca_frca_decree_allow_effect = yes
        }
        else = {
            remove_variable = frca_selector_from_119
            remove_variable = ca_faction_separatist
            remove_variable = ca_state_selection_to_playing
            set_variable = frca_reopen_119
            trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.119 popup = yes }
        }
        ai_chance = { base = 100 }
    }
}

## Honduras Beats to secessionists
# CAFM F6 (grupo 9): *Beats* en Honduras, ganan los federalistas. Lo lanza ca_fvs_resolve.
ca_central_america.81 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.81.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_fvs_beats_election_desc
				trigger = { scope:secession_country = { has_variable = ca_fvs_cause_election } }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_fvs_beats_coup_desc
				trigger = { scope:secession_country = { has_variable = ca_fvs_cause_coup } }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_fvs_beats_civil_war_desc
				trigger = { scope:secession_country = { has_variable = ca_fvs_cause_civil_war } }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_fvs_beats_morazan_desc
				trigger = { scope:secession_country = { has_variable = ca_fvs_cause_morazan } }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.81.d
				trigger = { always = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = ca_central_america.81.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_military_parade" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		c:HON ?= { has_variable = ca_fvs_result_beats }
	}

	immediate = {
		c:HON = { save_scope_as = secession_country }
	}

	# a. Reincorporarlo como provincia
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.81.a
		annex = scope:secession_country
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = hon VALUE = 3 }
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_up = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 50 }
		}
	}
	# b. Mantenerlo como estado miembro
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.81.b
		default_option = yes
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = hon VALUE = 3 }
		scope:secession_country = {
			add_modifier = { name = ca_federal_loyalty_restored months = 60 }
		}
		ca_frca_bar_minor_up = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -50 }
		}
	}
}

ca_central_america.82 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.82.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_fvs_falls_election_desc
				trigger = { scope:secession_country = { has_variable = ca_fvs_cause_election } }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_fvs_falls_coup_desc
				trigger = { scope:secession_country = { has_variable = ca_fvs_cause_coup } }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_fvs_falls_civil_war_desc
				trigger = { scope:secession_country = { has_variable = ca_fvs_cause_civil_war } }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_fvs_falls_assembly_desc
				trigger = { scope:secession_country = { has_variable = ca_fvs_cause_assembly } }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_fvs_falls_revolt_desc
				trigger = { scope:secession_country = { has_variable = ca_fvs_cause_revolt } }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.82.d
				trigger = { always = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = ca_central_america.82.f
	event_image = { video = "southamerica_war_civilians" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		c:HON ?= { has_variable = ca_fvs_result_falls }
	}

	immediate = {
		c:HON = { save_scope_as = secession_country }
	}

	# a. Aceptar: el estado sigue al paso 2
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.82.a
		default_option = yes
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = hon VALUE = 0 }
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -40 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
		}
	}
	# b. Preparar la reconquista (cuenta para la guerra federal .42)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.82.b
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = hon VALUE = 0 }
		s:STATE_HONDURAS ?= { add_claim = c:UCA }
		set_variable = frca_reconquest_hon
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 40 }
		}
	}
}

# =========================================================
# 1. CRISIS POLÍTICA Y SEPARATISMO
# =========================================================

# Aparición de líderes regionales ("Caudillo Emerges")
# CAFM F17: reescrito (grupo 16)
ca_central_america.88 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:caudillo_state
	title = ca_central_america.88.t
	desc = ca_central_america.88.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.88.f
	event_image = { video = "southamerica_aristocrats" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		ca_g16_eligible = yes
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_g16_88_cd }
		any_scope_state = {
			state_is_in_ca_central_america = yes
				turmoil >= 0.2
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_is_in_ca_central_america = yes
				turmoil >= 0.2
			}
			save_scope_as = caudillo_state
		}
		create_character = {
			interest_group = ig_armed_forces
			ideology = ideology_central_america_caudillo
			is_general = yes
			save_scope_as = regional_caudillo
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.88.a
		default_option = yes
		set_variable = { name = ca_g16_88_cd days = 1825 }
		scope:caudillo_state = { add_modifier = { name = ca_autonomous_caudillo_modifier months = 60 } }
		ca_g16_bar = { VALUE = -10 }
		scope:caudillo_state = { ca_g16_state_loyalty_down = yes }
		ca_frca_front_change = { FRONT = caudillos VALUE = 1 }
		ca_military_bar_change = { VALUE = -5 }
		set_variable = { name = ca_caudillo_state value = scope:caudillo_state }
		set_variable = { name = ca_caudillo_char value = scope:regional_caudillo }
		set_variable = { name = ca_caudillo_wait days = 270 }
		ai_chance = { base = 50
		modifier = { trigger = { has_global_variable = ca_world_historic } add = 20 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.88.b
		set_variable = { name = ca_g16_88_cd days = 1825 }
		scope:caudillo_state = { add_radicals_in_state = { value = 0.1 strata = lower } }
		ca_g16_bar = { VALUE = 10 }

ca_central_america.93 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:earthquake_state
	title = ca_central_america.93.t
	desc = ca_central_america.93.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.93.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_devastation" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		ca_g16_eligible = yes
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_g16_93_cd }
		any_scope_state = {
			state_is_in_ca_central_america = yes
				is_incorporated = yes
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_is_in_ca_central_america = yes
				is_incorporated = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = earthquake_state
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.93.a
		default_option = yes
		set_variable = { name = ca_g16_93_cd days = 1825 }
		add_treasury = { value = ca_money_medium multiply = -1 }
		add_modifier = { name = ca_federal_relief_bureaucracy_cost months = 24 }
		scope:earthquake_state = {
			add_devastation = 10
			add_modifier = { name = ca_federal_reconstruction months = 24 }
		}
		scope:earthquake_state = { ca_g16_state_loyalty_up = yes }
		ca_g16_bar = { VALUE = 10 }
		ai_chance = { base = 50
		modifier = { trigger = { gold_reserves < ca_money_medium } factor = 0.2 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.93.b
		set_variable = { name = ca_g16_93_cd days = 1825 }
		scope:earthquake_state = {
			add_devastation = 30
			add_radicals_in_state = { value = 0.2 strata = lower }
			add_modifier = { name = ca_abandoned_by_federal_gov months = 60 }
		}
		scope:earthquake_state = { ca_g16_state_loyalty_down = yes }
		ca_g16_bar = { VALUE = -20 }
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
}

# 3. Rebelión Campesina ("Peasant Uprising")

# CAFM F17: reescrito (grupo 16)
ca_central_america.95 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:rebellion_state
	title = ca_central_america.95.t
	desc = ca_central_america.95.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.95.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_vandalized_storefront" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		ca_g16_eligible = yes
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_g16_95_cd }
		NOT = {
			AND = {
				game_date >= 1837.1.1
				game_date < 1839.1.1
				OR = { c:UCA ?= this c:GUA ?= this }
			}
		}
		any_scope_state = {
			state_is_in_ca_central_america = yes
				turmoil > 0.15
				any_scope_pop = { is_pop_type = peasants }
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_is_in_ca_central_america = yes
				turmoil > 0.15
			}
			save_scope_as = rebellion_state
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.95.a
		default_option = yes
		set_variable = { name = ca_g16_95_cd days = 1825 }
		ig:ig_landowners ?= { add_modifier = { name = ca_ig_approval_down months = 60 } }
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= { add_modifier = { name = ca_ig_approval_up months = 60 } }
		scope:rebellion_state = { add_loyalists_in_state = { value = 0.1 strata = lower } }
		ca_coffee_bar_change = { VALUE = -5 }
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.95.b
		set_variable = { name = ca_g16_95_cd days = 1825 }
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { add_modifier = { name = ca_military_law_imposed months = 36 } }
		scope:rebellion_state = { add_radicals_in_state = { value = 0.1 strata = lower } }
		scope:rebellion_state = { ca_g16_state_loyalty_down = yes }
		ca_g16_bar = { VALUE = -10 }
		set_variable = { name = ca_peasant_state value = scope:rebellion_state }
		set_variable = { name = ca_peasant_wait days = 180 }
		ai_chance = { base = 60
		modifier = { trigger = { has_global_variable = ca_world_historic } add = 20 } }
	}
}

# Intervención Extranjera
# CAFM F17: reescrito (grupo 16)
ca_central_america.96 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.96.t
	desc = ca_central_america.96.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.96.f
	event_image = { video = "africa_diplomats_negotiating" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_diplomacy.dds"
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		ca_g16_eligible = yes
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_g16_96_cd }
		any_country = {
				OR = {
					c:GBR ?= this
					c:USA ?= this
					c:FRA ?= this
					c:SPA ?= this
				}
			is_subject = no
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					c:GBR ?= this
					c:USA ?= this
					c:FRA ?= this
					c:SPA ?= this
				}
				is_subject = no
			}
			save_scope_as = foreign_power
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.96.a
		default_option = yes
		set_variable = { name = ca_g16_96_cd days = 1825 }
		add_modifier = { name = ca_foreign_capital_injection months = 12 }
		set_owes_obligation_to = { country = scope:foreign_power setting = yes }
		ig:ig_industrialists ?= { add_modifier = { name = ca_ig_approval_up months = 60 } }
		ca_g16_bar = { VALUE = -10 }
		if = {
			limit = { scope:foreign_power = c:GBR }
		ca_frca_front_change = { FRONT = british VALUE = 1 }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.96.b
		set_variable = { name = ca_g16_96_cd days = 1825 }
		scope:foreign_power = { change_relations = { country = root value = -20 } }
		add_modifier = { name = ca_defiance_prestige months = 60 }
		ca_g16_bar = { VALUE = 10 }
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
}

ca_central_america.102 = {
	type = country_event
	event_image = { video = "europenorthamerica_capitalists_meeting" } # CAFM fix: sin imagen el evento muestra el cuadro magenta
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.102.t
	desc = ca_central_america.102.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.102.f
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_trade.dds"
	
	 

	# CAFM: solo para la UCA (antes saltaba en cualquier pais)
	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.102.a # "Abran las arcas del tesoro"
		# CAFM F7: opcion pro-federal: barra +10 y +10 por ciento de victoria federal (solo dinamico)

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } }

		}

		# CAFM F7: ayuda al bando de Los Altos (+10 por ciento de victoria federal, solo dinamico)
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } }
			c:ALT ?= { ca_cw_help_change = { VALUE = 1 } }
		}
		default_option = yes
		add_loyalists = { value = 0.2 interest_group = ig:ig_intelligentsia } # CAFM fix
		
		c:GUA ?= {
			change_relations = {
				country = root
				value = -50
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 70 } # F21
	}

	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.116.b # "No podemos dividir el territorio guatemalteco"
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes # CAFM F7
		set_variable = frca_alt_left_to_guatemala # frente conservador +1 si Los Altos cae
		
		ai_chance = { base = 30 } # F21
	}
}

# Inserto C: Los Mártires de Quetzaltenango (Resolución)
ca_central_america.118 = {
	type = country_event
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_sick_in_hospital" } # CAFM fix: sin imagen el evento muestra el cuadro magenta
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.118.t
	desc = ca_central_america.118.d
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"
	
	 

	# Si Los Altos cae (Evento 57)
	# CAFM: resolucion del capitulo de ALT. Llega por pulso mensual (zz_cafm_frca_on_actions.txt)
	# 180 dias despues de la guerra civil, cuando la UCA ya no esta en guerra.
	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		has_variable = ca_chapter_alt_started
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_chapter_alt_cooldown }
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_chapter_alt_resolved }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = ca_chapter_alt_resolved
		# CAFM F7 (grupo 13): el vencedor lo fija la historia o ca_civil_war_federal_chance;
		# la resolucion llama a ca_fvs_resolve (causa: guerra civil). C no mueve la barra.
		ca_cw_resolve = { CTAG = ALT NAME = los_altos BEATS = 56 FALLS = 57 }
		# Los Altos cae: Guatemala, ya separada, lo reabsorbe (Carrera, ene 1840)
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = ca_los_altos_separatists_won
				exists = c:ALT
				c:GUA ?= { NOT = { is_subject_of = c:UCA } }
			}
			c:GUA = { annex = c:ALT }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.118.a # "Una masacre imperdonable..."
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { has_variable = ca_los_altos_separatists_won } # En este contexto los separatistas son Guatemala aplastando a ALT
		add_radicals = { value = 0.2 interest_group = ig:ig_intelligentsia } # CAFM fix
		
		s:STATE_CHIAPAS = {
			every_scope_state = { add_modifier = { name = ca_tierra_arrasada_modifier months = 60 } } # CAFM fix
		}
		s:STATE_GUATEMALA = {
			every_scope_state = { add_modifier = { name = ca_tierra_arrasada_modifier months = 60 } } # CAFM fix
		}		
		ai_chance = { base = 1 } # F21
	}

	# Si Los Altos sobrevive (Evento 56)
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.118.b # "¡El baluarte del norte resiste!"
		trigger = { has_variable = ca_los_altos_loyalists_won }
		
		# CAFM F20: el Soconusco sigue en su diario (je_ca_soconusco, F14); la invitacion de Los Altos es ca_los_altos_soconusco_events.2
		ai_chance = { base = 1 } # F21
	}
}
# CAFM F4 (grupo 15): introduccion de la UCA. La lanza zzz_ca_uca.txt el dia 1.
# immediate: las 5 introducciones de los estados (solo informativas).
# a. Federalistas (historica) · b. Centralistas (solo jugador) · c. Jugar con un estado (.80)
# Si la UCA es IA: opcion a y sorteo 80/20 (frca_ai_historic / frca_ai_dynamic).
# "Mantenerse leal" en .80 reabre este evento (frca_reopen_119).
ca_central_america.119 = {
    type = country_event
    placement = root
    gui_window = ca_event_window_119
    title = ca_central_america.119.t
    desc = ca_central_america.119.d
    flavor = ca_central_america.119.f

    event_image = {
        video = "southamerica_aristocrats"
    }

    icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"
    on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
    duration = 3

    trigger = {
        exists = c:UCA
        this = c:UCA
        game_date < 1836.2.1
        OR = {
            NOT = { has_global_variable = ca_fired }
            has_variable = frca_reopen_119
        }
    }

    immediate = {
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_global_variable = ca_fired } }
            set_global_variable = ca_fired
            # Las 5 introducciones (sin opcion de jugar como el estado)
        }
        remove_variable = frca_reopen_119
    }

    # A. Federalistas (historica). La barra no cambia (75); los efectos del decreto estan en .79.
    option = {
        name = ca_central_america.119.a
        custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.119.a_guide_tt # P5: dificultad, objetivo y mecanicas
        default_option = yes
        ca_frca_choose_route = { ROUTE = federal }
		    trigger_event = { id = ca_guatemala_events.1 }
            trigger_event = { id = ca_el_salvador_events.1 }
            trigger_event = { id = ca_honduras_events.1 }
            trigger_event = { id = ca_nicaragua_events.1 }
            trigger_event = { id = ca_costa_rica_events.1 }
        ai_chance = { base = 100 }
    }

    # B. Centralistas (solo jugador)
    option = {
        name = ca_central_america.119.b
        custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.119.b_guide_tt # P5: dificultad, objetivo y mecanicas
        trigger = { is_ai = no }
        ca_frca_choose_route = { ROUTE = centralist }
        add_modifier = { name = unitary_stronghold }
		    trigger_event = { id = ca_guatemala_events.1 }
            trigger_event = { id = ca_el_salvador_events.1 }
            trigger_event = { id = ca_honduras_events.1 }
            trigger_event = { id = ca_nicaragua_events.1 }
            trigger_event = { id = ca_costa_rica_events.1 }
        ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
            add_modifier = { name = ca_ig_approval_strong_republic months = 24 }
        }
        ig:ig_landowners ?= {
            add_modifier = { name = ca_ig_disapproval_centralist_tyranny months = 24 }
        }
        ai_chance = { base = 0 }
    }

    # C. Jugar con un estado (solo jugador): abre el selector .80
    option = {
        name = ca_central_america.119.c
        custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.119.c_guide_tt # P5: dificultad, objetivo y mecanicas
        trigger = { is_ai = no }
        remove_variable = ca_faction_federal
        remove_variable = ca_faction_centralist
            trigger_event = { id = ca_guatemala_events.1 }
            trigger_event = { id = ca_el_salvador_events.1 }
            trigger_event = { id = ca_honduras_events.1 }
            trigger_event = { id = ca_nicaragua_events.1 }
            trigger_event = { id = ca_costa_rica_events.1 }
        set_variable = ca_faction_separatist
        set_variable = ca_state_selection_to_playing
        set_variable = frca_selector_from_119
        trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.80 popup = yes }
        ai_chance = { base = 0 }
    }
}
# CAFM F5 (grupo 15): el grito del sexto estado. Abre la cadena de Los Altos; ya no lo crea.
# IA historica: 2 feb 1838 (proclamacion del altiplano). Jugador e IA dinamica: desde 1837,
# con turmoil en Guatemala o terratenientes poderosos.
# Mano dura (b) suma ca_hidden_secession_var: vuelve al ano; a la 2.a vez, .4.
ca_central_america.120 = {
	type = country_event
	event_image = { video = "southamerica_aristocrats" }
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.120.t
	desc = ca_central_america.120.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.120.f
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		has_journal_entry = je_historic_central_america_falls_apart
		has_global_variable = ca_los_altos_question
		exists = s:STATE_GUATEMALA.region_state:UCA
		NOT = { exists = c:ALT }
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_grito_sexto_estado_fired }
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_grito_cooldown }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				ca_frca_ai_historic = yes
				game_date >= 1838.2.2
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes }
				game_date >= 1837.1.1
				OR = {
					s:STATE_GUATEMALA = {
						any_scope_state = {
							owner = c:UCA
							turmoil > ca_loyalty_turmoil_threshold
						}
					}
					ig:ig_landowners ?= { is_powerful = yes }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = { name = ca_grito_cooldown days = 365 }
	}

	# a. Llevarlo al Congreso (historica). Solo ruta federal.
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.120.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { ca_frca_route_federal = yes }
		set_variable = ca_grito_sexto_estado_fired
		trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.2 days = 15 }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
		}
	}

	# b. Mano dura
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.120.b
		ca_frca_loyalty_up = { TAG = gua }
		add_radicals = { value = 0.15 interest_group = ig:ig_intelligentsia }
		add_radicals = { value = 0.15 interest_group = ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie }
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = ca_hidden_secession_var } }
			set_variable = { name = ca_hidden_secession_var value = 0 }
		}
		change_variable = { name = ca_hidden_secession_var add = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { var:ca_hidden_secession_var >= 2 }
			set_variable = ca_grito_sexto_estado_fired
			set_variable = frca_alt_rebellion_pending
			trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.4 days = 30 }
		}
		else = {
			custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.120.b_tt
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 60 }
		}
	}

	# c. Que Guatemala lo resuelva: el altiplano se alza en 30 dias
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.120.c
		set_variable = ca_grito_sexto_estado_fired
		s:STATE_GUATEMALA.region_state:UCA ?= {
			add_modifier = { name = ca_massive_agitation_modifier months = 12 }
		}
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		set_variable = frca_alt_rebellion_pending
		trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.4 days = 30 }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 10 }
		}
	}
}

# CAFM F5 (grupo 15): el peso de Guatemala (reformas de Galvez). 1836-1837, una vez,
# con Guatemala todavia dentro de la Federacion. Ya no lanza .3 ni .4.
ca_central_america.121 = {
	type = country_event
	event_image = { video = "southamerica_aristocrats" }
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.121.t
	desc = ca_central_america.121.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.121.f
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		game_date >= 1836.3.1
		game_date < 1838.1.1
		exists = s:STATE_GUATEMALA.region_state:UCA
		ca_frca_loyalty_ge = { TAG = gua VALUE = 1 }
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_peso_guatemala_fired }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = ca_peso_guatemala_fired
	}

	# a. Reformas liberales (historica). Hace mas probable la revuelta de Carrera (.110).
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.121.a
		default_option = yes
		ca_frca_loyalty_down = { TAG = gua }
		set_variable = ca_gua_reforms_pushed
		ig:ig_landowners ?= { add_modifier = { name = ca_ig_approval_down months = 24 } }
		ig:ig_devout ?= { add_modifier = { name = ca_ig_approval_down months = 24 } }
		ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { add_modifier = { name = ca_ig_approval_up months = 24 } }
		ca_frca_bar_minor_up = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -30 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
		}
	}

	# b. Mantener a las elites de Guatemala
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.121.b
		ca_frca_loyalty_up = { TAG = gua }
		ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { add_modifier = { name = ca_ig_approval_down months = 24 } }
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 30 }
		}
	}

	# c. El Congreso no llega a un acuerdo
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.121.c
		add_modifier = { name = ca_paralisis_federal months = 12 }
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
}

# CAFM F5 (grupo 15): se elimina el contenido viejo de .122 ("La secesion de Los Altos",
# duplicaba .4 y .78). El ID queda para "La Federacion se desvanece" (desenlace D2, F8).

# =====================================================================
# CAFM F6 - eventos nuevos: .64 (levantamiento armado en Nicaragua) y .99 (independencia de El Salvador)
# =====================================================================
# CAFM F6 (grupo 12): levantamiento armado en Nicaragua. Solo camino dinamico: llega desde la
# declaracion en armas del estado, la negativa de la UCA o la mano dura del paso 1.
ca_central_america.64 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.64.t
	desc = ca_central_america.64.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.64.f
	event_image = { video = "southamerica_war_civilians" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		has_variable = frca_nic_armed_pending
		c:NIC ?= { is_subject_of = c:UCA }
	}

	immediate = {
		remove_variable = frca_nic_armed_pending
		c:NIC = { save_scope_as = secession_country }
	}

	# a. Aplastar el levantamiento: guerra de independencia
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.64.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:secession_country = {
			create_diplomatic_play = {
				target_country = c:UCA
				type = dp_independence
			}
		}
		set_variable = frca_indep_war_nic
		set_variable = { name = frca_indep_war_grace_nic days = 90 }
		custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.armed_war_tt
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 30 }
		}
	}
	# b. Dejarlos ir
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.64.b
		ca_frca_recognize_independence = { TAG = nic CTAG = NIC NAME = nicaragua RELATIONS = -20 }
		ca_frca_bar_major_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -10 }
		}
	}
	# c. Negociar: vuelve a ser miembro con lealtad 1 y concesiones
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.64.c
		ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = nic VALUE = 1 }
		scope:secession_country = {
			add_modifier = { name = ca_secession_concessions months = 24 }
			remove_variable = ca_independence_declared
		}
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -20 }
		}
	}
}

# CAFM F6 (grupo 11): respuesta de la UCA a la independencia de El Salvador (paso 2).
# Historico (IA 80 %): 1841.1.30. Dinamico: peticion pacifica del estado (.52).
ca_central_america.99 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.99.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.99.d_petition
				trigger = { has_variable = frca_els_by_petition }
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.99.d
				trigger = { always = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = ca_central_america.99.f
	event_image = { video = "gfx/event_pictures/southamerica_aristocrats.bk2" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_map.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		NOT = { has_variable = frca_els_independence_done }
		c:ELS ?= {
			is_subject_of = c:UCA
			is_subject_type = subject_type_ca_dependency
		}
		ca_frca_loyalty_le = { TAG = els VALUE = 0 }
		# El Salvador solo se separa si la capital de la UCA no esta en San Salvador (grupo 11)
		c:UCA ?= { capital = { NOT = { state_region = s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR } } }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				ca_frca_ai_historic = yes
				game_date >= 1841.1.30
			}
			has_variable = frca_els_independence_request
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = frca_els_independence_done
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = frca_els_independence_request }
			set_variable = { name = frca_els_by_petition days = 30 }
		}
		remove_variable = frca_els_independence_request
		c:ELS = { save_scope_as = secession_country }
	}

	# a. Reconocer la independencia
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.99.a
		default_option = yes
		ca_frca_recognize_independence = { TAG = els CTAG = ELS NAME = elsalvador RELATIONS = 20 }
		ca_frca_bar_moderate_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = -40 }
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_ai_historic = yes } add = 1000 }
		}
	}
	# b. Negar: el estado elige someterse o alzarse en armas
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.99.b
		s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR ?= { add_claim = c:UCA }
		scope:secession_country = {
			change_relations = { country = c:UCA value = -50 }
			set_variable = ca_independence_denied
			trigger_event = { id = ca_central_america.52 days = 7 }
		}
		remove_variable = frca_els_independence_done
		ca_frca_bar_minor_down = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = { trigger = { ca_frca_route_centralist = yes } add = 40 }
		}
	}
}

# CAFM F8 - eventos nuevos: .77 (El exilio de Morazan) y .122 (La Federacion se desvanece)
# CAFM F8 (grupo 8, grupo 14): "El exilio de Morazan". IA historica: 8 abr 1840, si no hay
# frca_won_war (derrota en Ciudad de Guatemala, 18-19 mar 1840; sale por La Libertad). Tambien
# lo lanza .115 a (los separatistas ganan El Salvador). Lleva a D1.
ca_central_america.77 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.77.t
	desc = ca_central_america.77.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.77.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_devastation" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_portrait.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		ca_frca_journal_active = yes
		NOT = { has_variable = frca_morazan_exiled }
		NOT = { has_variable = frca_won_war }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				ca_frca_ai_historic = yes
				game_date >= 1840.4.8
			}
			has_variable = frca_morazan_exile_pending
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.77.a
		default_option = yes
		remove_variable = frca_morazan_exile_pending
		set_variable = frca_morazan_exiled
		set_global_variable = ca_morazan_exiled
		global_var:cafm_hc_morazan ?= {
			set_variable = morazan_exiliado
		}
		custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.77.a_tt
	}
}

# CAFM F8 (D2): "La Federacion se desvanece". Lo lanza el timeout del diario (1845 sin A ni B y sin
# la Dieta). Reparte el territorio segun la lealtad: subditos e impropios con lealtad <= 1 se van
# sin guerra; con 2 o mas estados nucleo, "Federacion menguada"; si no, la UCA pasa a ser el
# estado que le queda. Relaciones +30, sin devastacion y con reclamaciones de reunificacion.
ca_central_america.122 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.122.t
	desc = ca_central_america.122.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.122.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_signed_contract" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_map.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		has_variable = frca_outcome_d2
		NOT = { has_global_variable = ca_frca_d2_fired }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_global_variable = ca_frca_d2_fired
	}

	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.122.a
		default_option = yes
		ca_frca_outcome_d2 = yes
	}
}

# CAFM F10: frente british, escalada. Fase IV, con la presion que lo sube (diario §6).
# Una vez al ano como mucho. a contener (costo, frente igual) · b resistir (frente +1).
ca_central_america.150 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.150.t
	desc = ca_central_america.150.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.150.f
	event_image = { video = "europenorthamerica_london_center" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_trade.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		ca_frca_journal_active = yes
		ca_frca_phase_ge = { VALUE = 4 }
		NOT = { ca_frca_front_ge = { FRONT = british VALUE = 3 } }
		NOT = { has_variable = frca_front_british_cd }
		OR = {
			has_journal_entry = je_ca_british_debt
			c:MKT ?= { is_subject_of = c:GBR }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = { name = frca_front_british_cd days = 365 }
	}

	# a. Contener la presion
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.150.a
		default_option = yes
		add_treasury = { value = ca_money_medium multiply = -1 }
		add_modifier = { name = ca_frca_british_concessions months = 24 }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = { trigger = { gold_reserves < 5000 } add = -40 }
		}
	}
	# b. Resistir: el frente empeora
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.150.b
		ca_frca_front_change = { FRONT = british VALUE = 1 }
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
}

# CAFM F10: frente british, crisis. El frente llega a Perdido: se aplica lo que se pierde (diario §6).
ca_central_america.151 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.151.t
	desc = ca_central_america.151.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.151.f
	event_image = { video = "europenorthamerica_london_center" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_trade.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		ca_frca_journal_active = yes
		ca_frca_front_ge = { FRONT = british VALUE = 3 }
		NOT = { has_variable = frca_front_british_lost_fired }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = frca_front_british_lost_fired
	}

	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.151.a
		default_option = yes
		# La Mosquitia y la costa caribe de Nicaragua quedan bajo protectorado britanico
		if = {
			limit = { exists = c:MKT }
			s:STATE_NICARAGUA = {
				set_owner_of_provinces = {
					country = c:MKT
					provinces = { x745F00 x107080 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:GBR
					c:MKT = { NOT = { is_subject_of = c:GBR } }
				}
				c:GBR = { create_diplomatic_pact = { country = c:MKT type = protectorate } }
			}
		}
		c:GBR ?= { change_relations = { country = c:UCA value = -30 } }
	}
}

# CAFM F10: frente mexican, crisis. El frente llega a Perdido: se aplica lo que se pierde (diario §6).
ca_central_america.153 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.153.t
	desc = ca_central_america.153.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.153.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_politicians_arguing" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_map.dds"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		ca_frca_journal_active = yes
		ca_frca_front_ge = { FRONT = mexican VALUE = 3 }
		NOT = { has_variable = frca_front_mexican_lost_fired }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = frca_front_mexican_lost_fired
	}

	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.153.a
		default_option = yes
		# El Soconusco (o la parte de Chiapas que tenga la UCA) pasa a Mexico
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:MEX
				exists = c:CIP
			}
			c:MEX = { annex = c:CIP }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:MEX
				exists = s:STATE_CHIAPAS.region_state:UCA
			}
			s:STATE_CHIAPAS.region_state:UCA = { set_state_owner = c:MEX }
		}
		set_variable = frca_cip_left_federation
		remove_variable = frca_loyalty_cip
		c:MEX ?= { change_relations = { country = c:UCA value = -30 } }
	}
}

		add_modifier = { name = ca_frca_military_coup months = 24 }

			limit = { ca_frca_loyalty_ge = { TAG = hon VALUE = 2 } }
			ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = hon VALUE = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { ca_frca_loyalty_ge = { TAG = nic VALUE = 2 } }
			ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = nic VALUE = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { ca_frca_loyalty_ge = { TAG = els VALUE = 2 } }
			ca_frca_loyalty_set = { TAG = els VALUE = 1 }
		}
	}
}

ca_central_america.159 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.159.t
	desc = ca_central_america.159.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.159.f
	event_image = { video = "africa_diplomats_negotiating" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_diplomacy.dds"
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		has_journal_entry = je_ca_confederation
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_conf_159_fired }
		any_country = {
			OR = { c:GUA ?= this c:COS ?= this }
			is_subject = no
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = ca_conf_159_fired
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				OR = { c:GUA ?= this c:COS ?= this }
				ca_conf_can_be_invited = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_ai = yes NOT = { c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_ai_historic } } }
				random = {
					chance = ca_conf_invite_chance
					ca_conf_join = yes
					set_variable = { name = ca_conf_joined_159 days = 30 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.159.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = {
			NOT = { any_country = { has_variable = ca_conf_joined_159 } }
		}
		ca_conf_cohesion_change = { VALUE = -5 }
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.159.b
		trigger = {
			any_country = { has_variable = ca_conf_joined_159 }
		}
		ca_conf_cohesion_change = { VALUE = 5 }
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
}

ca_central_america.160 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.160.t
	desc = ca_central_america.160.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.160.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_military_parade" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		has_journal_entry = je_ca_confederation
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_conf_160_fired }
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_conf_grace }
		any_subject_or_below = {
			is_subject_type = subject_type_ca_confederative_partner
			exists = ruler
			OR = {
				ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { is_powerful = yes }
				AND = {
					c:ELS ?= this
					game_date >= 1844.2.1
					c:UCA ?= { has_variable = frca_ai_historic }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		ordered_subject_or_below = {
			limit = {
				is_subject_type = subject_type_ca_confederative_partner
				exists = ruler
			}
			order_by = {
				value = 0
				if = { limit = { c:ELS ?= this } add = 10 }
				if = { limit = { ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { is_powerful = yes } } add = 20 }
			}
			save_scope_as = ca_conf_rebel
			ruler = { save_scope_as = ca_conf_caudillo }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.160.a
		default_option = yes
		set_variable = ca_conf_160_fired
		ca_conf_cohesion_change = { VALUE = -10 }
		ca_frca_front_change = { FRONT = caudillos VALUE = 1 }
		scope:ca_conf_rebel = { change_relations = { country = root value = 20 } }
		ai_chance = { base = 40
		modifier = { trigger = { has_variable = frca_ai_historic } add = 200 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.160.b
		set_variable = ca_conf_160_fired
		set_variable = { name = ca_conf_caudillo_war days = 730 }
		ca_conf_cohesion_change = { VALUE = -5 }
		scope:ca_conf_rebel = { change_relations = { country = root value = -40 } }
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.160.c
		set_variable = ca_conf_160_fired
		add_treasury = { value = ca_money_medium multiply = -1 }
		ai_chance = { base = 30
		modifier = { trigger = { gold_reserves < ca_money_medium } factor = 0 } }
	}
}

	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.161.t

	}

			NOT = { has_variable = frca_ai_historic }
		}

	title = ca_central_america.163.t
	desc = ca_central_america.163.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.163.f

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		has_journal_entry = je_ca_patria_grande
		NOT = { has_global_variable = ca_national_war_fired }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				has_global_variable = ca_world_historic
				game_date >= 1856.3.1
				game_date < 1858.1.1
			}
			any_country = {
				NOT = { ca_is_ca_national_country = yes }
				any_scope_state = {
					OR = {
						state_region = s:STATE_NICARAGUA
						state_region = s:STATE_HONDURAS
						state_region = s:STATE_SAN_SALVADOR
						state_region = s:STATE_COSTA_RICA
						state_region = s:STATE_GUATEMALA
					}
					is_capital = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.163.a
		default_option = yes
		set_global_variable = ca_national_war_fired
		add_treasury = { value = ca_money_medium multiply = -1 }
		ca_unity_change = { VALUE = 20 }
		add_modifier = { name = ca_national_war_mod months = 24 }
		ai_chance = { base = 70 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.163.b
		set_global_variable = ca_national_war_fired
		ca_unity_change = { VALUE = -5 }
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
}

ca_central_america.164 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.164.t
	desc = ca_central_america.164.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.164.f
	event_image = { video = "europenorthamerica_before_the_battle" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		has_journal_entry = je_ca_patria_grande
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_union_campaign_fired }
		exists = ruler
		OR = {
			AND = {
				has_global_variable = ca_world_historic
				is_ai = yes
				c:GUA ?= this
				game_date >= 1885.2.28
			}
			AND = {
				var:ca_unity >= 60
				ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { is_powerful = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		ruler = { save_scope_as = ca_unionist_ruler }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.164.a
		default_option = yes
		set_variable = ca_union_campaign_fired
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = yes has_global_variable = ca_world_historic }
			set_variable = ca_barrios_failed
			ca_unity_change = { VALUE = -30 }
			custom_tooltip = ca_central_america.164.historic_tt
		}
		else = {
			ca_unity_change = { VALUE = 10 }
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					ca_is_ca_national_country = yes
					is_subject = no
					NOT = { this = root }
				}
				every_scope_state = {
					state_region = { add_claim = root }
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 60 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.164.b
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_global_variable = ca_world_historic }
		}
		set_variable = ca_union_campaign_fired
		ca_unity_change = { VALUE = -5 }
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
}

	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.165.t
	desc = ca_central_america.165.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.165.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_signed_contract" }

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		has_journal_entry = je_ca_patria_grande
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_patria_mayor_fired }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				has_global_variable = ca_world_historic
				game_date >= 1895.6.20
			}
			AND = {
				var:ca_unity >= 50
				any_country = {
					ca_is_ca_national_country = yes
					is_subject = no
					NOT = { this = root }
					relations:root >= relations_threshold:friendly
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.165.a
		default_option = yes
		set_variable = ca_patria_mayor_fired
		set_variable = ca_patria_mayor_signed
		ca_unity_change = { VALUE = 15 }

		every_country = {
			limit = {
				ca_is_ca_national_country = yes
				is_subject = no
				NOT = { this = root }
				relations:root >= relations_threshold:friendly
			}
			change_relations = { country = root value = 20 }
			set_variable = ca_patria_mayor_member
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 70 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.165.b
		set_variable = ca_patria_mayor_fired
		ca_unity_change = { VALUE = -10 }
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }

ca_central_america.130 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:ca_dyn_state
	title = ca_central_america.130.t
	desc = ca_central_america.130.d
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_devastation" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_fire.dds"
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		ca_g16_eligible = yes
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_g16_130_cd }
		any_scope_state = {
			state_is_in_ca_central_america = yes
				is_incorporated = yes
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_is_in_ca_central_america = yes
				is_incorporated = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = ca_dyn_state
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.130.a
		default_option = yes
		set_variable = { name = ca_g16_130_cd days = 1825 }
		add_treasury = { value = ca_money_small multiply = -1 }
		scope:ca_dyn_state = { add_devastation = 10 }
		scope:ca_dyn_state = { ca_g16_state_loyalty_up = yes }
		ca_g16_bar = { VALUE = 10 }
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.130.b
		set_variable = { name = ca_g16_130_cd days = 1825 }
		scope:ca_dyn_state = {
			add_devastation = 25
			add_radicals_in_state = { value = 0.1 strata = lower }
		}
		ca_g16_bar = { VALUE = -10 }
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
}

			c:NIC ?= this

			c:COS ?= this
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.140.a
		default_option = yes
		set_variable = { name = ca_g16_140_cd days = 1825 }
		ig:ig_landowners ?= { add_modifier = { name = ca_ig_approval_up months = 24 } }
		add_radicals = { value = small_radicals strata = middle }
		ca_capital_city_axis = { A = liberal B = conservative }
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.140.b
		set_variable = { name = ca_g16_140_cd days = 1825 }
		ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie ?= { add_modifier = { name = ca_ig_approval_up months = 24 } }
		ig:ig_landowners ?= { add_modifier = { name = ca_ig_approval_down months = 24 } }
		add_modifier = { name = ca_capital_moved months = 24 }
		ca_capital_city_axis = { A = conservative B = liberal }
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.140.c
		set_variable = { name = ca_g16_140_cd days = 1825 }
		add_modifier = { name = ca_capital_alternating months = 60 }
		remove_variable = ca_capital_liberal_city
		remove_variable = ca_capital_conservative_city
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
}

		modifier = { trigger = { is_at_war = yes } factor = 0 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.169.c
		set_variable = ca_caudillo_c3_done

		var:ca_caudillo_char ?= { transfer_character = root }
		annex = var:ca_caudillo_city

	}

	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.170.t
	desc = ca_central_america.170.d

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		has_variable = ca_caudillo_city
		exists = var:ca_caudillo_city
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_caudillo_city_young }
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_caudillo_c4_done }
		var:ca_caudillo_city = { is_subject = no }
		OR = {
			legitimacy < 40
			is_at_war = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.170.a
		default_option = yes

			create_diplomatic_play = {
				name = ca_caudillo_campaign_play
				target_country = var:ca_caudillo_city
				type = dp_annex_war

		}

		ai_chance = { base = 60 }

			transfer_character = root
			set_character_as_ruler = yes
		}
		annex = var:ca_caudillo_city
		set_variable = jefe_supremo_var
		set_variable = { name = recent_coup_or_revolution_var days = 730 } # F20
		ca_frca_front_change = { FRONT = caudillos VALUE = 1 }
		ca_military_bar_change = { VALUE = -20 }
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
