ca_central_america.171 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.171.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.171.d
				trigger = {
					exists = var:ca_caudillo_city
					var:ca_caudillo_city = { is_subject = no }
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.171.d2
				trigger = {
					always = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = ca_central_america.171.f
	event_image = { video = "unspecific_politicians_arguing" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_portrait.dds"
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		has_variable = ca_caudillo_city
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_caudillo_done }
		OR = {
			NOT = { exists = var:ca_caudillo_city }
			var:ca_caudillo_city = { is_subject_of = root }
			NOT = { has_variable = ca_caudillo_city_old }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.171.a
		default_option = yes
		set_variable = ca_caudillo_done
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = var:ca_caudillo_city
				var:ca_caudillo_city = { is_subject = no }
			}
		ca_frca_front_change = { FRONT = caudillos VALUE = 1 }
		}
		else = {
		ca_frca_front_change = { FRONT = caudillos VALUE = -1 }
			ca_military_bar_change = { VALUE = 10 }
		}
		remove_variable = ca_caudillo_char
		remove_variable = ca_caudillo_state
		ai_chance = { base = 1 }
	}
}

				type = dp_annex_war
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 60 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.174.b
		set_variable = ca_peasant_p4_done
		add_modifier = { name = ca_peasant_guerrilla months = 36 }
		var:ca_peasant_state ?= {
			random_neighbouring_state = {
				limit = { owner = root }
				add_radicals_in_state = { value = 0.1 strata = lower }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
}

ca_central_america.175 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.175.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.175.d
				trigger = {
					exists = var:ca_peasant_city
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = ca_central_america.175.d2
				trigger = {
					always = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = ca_central_america.175.f
	event_image = { video = "asia_farmers_market" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_agriculture.dds"
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		has_variable = ca_peasant_city
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_peasant_done }
		OR = {
			NOT = { exists = var:ca_peasant_city }
			NOT = { has_variable = ca_peasant_city_old }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.175.a
		default_option = yes
		set_variable = ca_peasant_done
		if = {
			limit = { exists = var:ca_peasant_city }
			var:ca_peasant_city = { change_relations = { country = root value = 30 } }
		}
		remove_variable = ca_peasant_state
		ai_chance = { base = 1 }
	}
}

# CAFM P4 (punto 5): los britanicos desembarcan desde la Mosquitia (control de Bocas bajo).
# Lo lanza ca_bocas_monthly.
ca_central_america.176 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = ca_central_america.176.t
	desc = ca_central_america.176.d
	flavor = ca_central_america.176.f
	event_image = { video = "europenorthamerica_before_the_battle" }
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		this = c:UCA
		exists = c:GBR
		ca_bocas_held_by_uca = yes
		NOT = { ca_bocas_control_ge = { VALUE = 30 } }
		NOT = { has_variable = ca_bocas_landing_cd }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = { name = ca_bocas_landing_cd years = 5 }
	}

	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.176.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = { gold_reserves >= ca_money_medium }
		add_treasury = { value = ca_money_medium multiply = -1 }
		ca_bocas_control_change = { VALUE = 10 }
		ai_chance = { base = 60 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ca_central_america.176.b
		ca_bocas_control_change = { VALUE = -20 }
		ca_frca_front_change = { FRONT = british VALUE = 1 }
		c:GBR = { save_scope_as = ca_gbr }
		change_relations = { country = scope:ca_gbr value = -15 }
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
}
